    87	  mutable prog_lo : int; mutable str_lo : int; mutable str_hi : int;
    88	  mutable hout_lat : bool; mutable smp_lat : bool;
    89	  mutable last_fetch : (int * int * int) option;   (* for diagnostics *)
    90	}
    91	
    92	let rep4 b = if b <> 0 then 15 else 0
    93	let bit v i = (v lsr i) land 1
    94	let u32 v = v land 0xFFFF_FFFF
    95	let reg32 t a = t.regs.(a) lor (t.regs.(a + 1) lsl 8) lor (t.regs.(a + 2) lsl 16) lor (t.regs.(a + 3) lsl 24)
    96	
    97	(* ---- block configurations from the registers ---- *)
    98	
    99	let streamer_cfg t : SM.cfg =
   100	  let r = t.regs in
   101	  { period = r.(r_str_per) lor ((r.(r_str_per + 1) land 15) lsl 8); width = (r.(r_str_per + 1) lsr 4) land 7;
   102	    od_mask = r.(r_str_pins) land 15; idle_out = (r.(r_str_pins) lsr 4) land 15; idle_oe = r.(r_str_idle_oe) land 15 }
   103	
   104	let sampler_cfg t : PM.cfg =
   105	  let r = t.regs in
   106	  { clocked = bit r.(r_smp_per + 1) 7 = 1; period = r.(r_smp_per) lor ((r.(r_smp_per + 1) land 15) lsl 8);
   107	    width = (r.(r_smp_per + 1) lsr 4) land 7; trig_pin = (r.(r_smp_off + 1) lsr 4) land 3;
   108	    trig_val = bit r.(r_smp_off + 1) 6; offset = r.(r_smp_off) lor ((r.(r_smp_off + 1) land 15) lsl 8);
   109	    frame_len = r.(r_smp_frame) }
   110	
   111	let es_cfg t : ES.cfg =
   112	  let r = t.regs in
   113	  { mode = (match r.(r_es_mode) land 3 with 0 -> ES.Manchester | 2 -> ES.Nrz | _ -> ES.Biphase_mark);
   114	    invert = bit r.(r_es_mode) 2 = 1;
   115	    holdoff = r.(r_es_holdoff) lor ((r.(r_es_holdoff + 1) land 3) lsl 8);
   116	    timeout = r.(r_es_timeout) lor ((r.(r_es_timeout + 1) land 3) lsl 8);
   117	    offset = r.(r_es_offset); period = r.(r_es_period) }
   118	
   119	(* The CRC unit's model takes a width; the RTL takes a mask. The host must write a mask of the
   120	   form 2^w - 1 (README); the width is then the number of ones. *)
   121	let crc_cfg t : CRC.cfg =
   122	  let mask = reg32 t r_crc_mask in
   123	  let rec ones v = if v = 0 then 0 else (v land 1) + ones (v lsr 1) in
   124	  { width = max 1 (ones mask); poly = reg32 t r_crc_poly; init = reg32 t r_crc_init;
   125	    reflected = bit t.regs.(r_crc_ctrl) 0 = 1; xorout = reg32 t r_crc_xorout; residue = reg32 t r_crc_residue }
   126	
   127	let matcher_cfg t : MM.cfg =
   128	  { t = Array.init MM.n (fun i -> t.chain.(2 * i + 1)); m = Array.init MM.n (fun i -> t.chain.(2 * i));
   129	    thr = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 5 (fun k -> t.chain.(32 + k) lsl k)) }
   130	
   131	let matcher_now t =
   132	  let c = matcher_cfg t and x k = t.mhist.(k) in
   133	  (MM.y_at c x t.mtime, MM.hit_at c x t.mtime)
   134	
   135	let boot t = Array.init 4 (fun th -> ((t.regs.(r_boot_page) lsr (2 * th)) land 3, t.regs.(r_boot_pc th)))
   136	
   137	(* coverage of chip-level events, for the lockstep's report (not part of the behaviour) *)
   138	let cov : (string, int) Hashtbl.t = Hashtbl.create 64
   139	let hit name = Hashtbl.replace cov name (1 + Option.value ~default:0 (Hashtbl.find_opt cov name))
   140	
   141	let es_zero : ES.out = { bit = 0; valid = false; burst_end = false; overrun = false }
   142	
   143	(* The edge sampler's bit output is defined only while its valid is high; the glue holds the
   144	   last recovered bit, so pads, flags and the sampler see a level. *)
   145	let es_level t = if t.es_out.valid then t.es_out.bit else t.es_last
   146	
   147	let create ?(cfg = default_config) () =
   148	  let regs = Array.init reg_space reset_value in
   149	  let t =
   150	    { cfg; regs; err_mem = false; hin_ovf = false; hout_ovf = false; act_drop = false;
   151	      prog = Array.make cfg.prog_words 0; bank = Array.make bank_bytes 0;
   152	      seq = Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:(Array.make 4 (0, 0)) ~bank:(Array.make bank_bytes 0);
   153	      hostin = Queue.create (); hostout = Queue.create (); out_pend = None; push_pend = None;
   154	      send_hi = Array.make 4 false; hold_st = Array.make 4 0; hold_w = Array.make 4 0;
   155	      pe = UM.create ~layout:cfg.layout ();
   156	      str = SM.create { period = 1; width = 1; od_mask = 0; idle_out = 0; idle_oe = 0 };
   157	      smp = PM.create { clocked = false; period = 1; width = 1; trig_pin = 0; trig_val = 0; offset = 0; frame_len = 0 };
   158	      es = ES.init (); es_out = es_zero; es_last = 0; crc = 0; chain = Array.make 37 0; mhist = Array.make 1024 0;
   159	      mtime = 0; nco_acc = 0; nco_out = 0; prev_s = 0; prev_r = 0; nphase = 0; hi_nib = 0; pst = Idle;
   160	      op = 0; tgt = 0; addr = 0; remaining = 0; rbuf = 0; rphase = 0; pend_action = None;
   161	      pend_fetch = None; prog_lo = 0; str_lo = 0; str_hi = 0; hout_lat = false; smp_lat = false; last_fetch = None }
   162	  in
   163	  t.seq <- Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:(boot t) ~bank:t.bank;
   164	  t.str <- SM.create (streamer_cfg t);
   165	  t.smp <- PM.create (sampler_cfg t);
   166	  t
   167	
   168	(* ---- outputs: from the state at the start of the clock ---- *)
   169	
   170	type outputs = { pad_nib : int array; uio_oe : int }
   171	
   172	let taps t = (UM.state t.pe).taps
   173	
   174	let source t (taps : US.tap array) src =
   175	  if src < 8 then ((t.seq.pin_sub lsr (4 * src)) land 15, bit t.seq.pin_oe src)
   176	  else if src < 12 then (rep4 (bit t.str.out (src - 8)), bit t.str.oe (src - 8))
   177	  else if src = src_nco_quarter then (t.nco_out land 15, 1)
   178	  else if src = src_nco_half then ((t.nco_out lsr 4) land 15, 1)
   179	  else if src = src_nco_grid then ((t.nco_out lsr 8) land 15, 1)
   180	  else if src >= 16 && src < 20 then (rep4 (Bool.to_int taps.(src - 16).tf), 1)
   181	  else if src >= 20 && src < 24 then (rep4 (taps.(src - 20).td land 1), 1)
   182	  else if src = src_es_bit then (rep4 (es_level t), 1)
   183	  else (0, 1)
   184	
   185	let outputs t =
   186	  let taps = taps t in
   187	  let pad_nib = Array.make n_pads 0 and uio_oe = ref 0 in
   188	  List.iter
   189	    (fun p ->
   190	      let sel = t.regs.(r_padsel p) in
   191	      let nib, oe = source t taps (sel land 31) in
   192	      let nib = if bit sel 5 = 1 then nib lxor 15 else nib in
   193	      if p < 8 then pad_nib.(p) <- (if oe = 1 then nib else rep4 (bit sel 6))
   194	      else begin
   195	        pad_nib.(p) <- nib;
   196	        if oe = 1 then uio_oe := !uio_oe lor (1 lsl (p - 8))
   197	      end)
   198	    general_out_pads;
   199	  (* host data: the current read nibble, driven while the host's read request is seen *)
   200	  let rnib = if t.rphase = 0 then t.rbuf lsr 4 else t.rbuf land 15 in
   201	  for k = 0 to 3 do pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k) <- rep4 (bit rnib k) done;
   202	  if t.prev_r = 1 then uio_oe := !uio_oe lor 0xF;
   203	  { pad_nib; uio_oe = !uio_oe }
   204	
   205	let flags t (taps : US.tap array) =
   206	  let src s =
   207	    if s < 4 then taps.(s).tf
   208	    else if s < 8 then taps.(s - 4).tv
   209	    else if s = fl_es_valid then t.es_out.valid
   210	    else if s = fl_es_bit then es_level t = 1
   211	    else if s = fl_es_in_burst then t.es.in_burst
   212	    else if s = fl_es_burst_end then t.es_out.burst_end
   213	    else if s = fl_crc_check then t.crc = (crc_cfg t).residue
   214	    else if s = fl_match_hit then snd (matcher_now t)
   215	    else if s = fl_stream_full then SM.full t.str
   216	    else if s = fl_sample_valid then not (Queue.is_empty t.smp.fifo)
   217	    else if s = fl_nco then bit t.nco_out 0 = 1
   218	    else false
   219	  in
   220	  let v = ref 0 in
   221	  for f = 0 to 15 do if src (t.regs.(r_flagsel f) land 31) then v := !v lor (1 lsl f) done;
   222	  !v
   223	
   224	(* ---- host-link reads ---- *)
   225	
   226	let run t = bit t.regs.(r_ctrl) 0 = 1
   227	let held t = bit t.regs.(r_ctrl) 1 = 1
   228	
   229	let status t =
   230	  Bool.to_int t.err_mem lor (Bool.to_int t.hin_ovf lsl 1) lor (Bool.to_int t.hout_ovf lsl 2)
   231	  lor (Bool.to_int (Queue.length t.hostin >= hostin_depth) lsl 3)
   232	  lor (Bool.to_int (not (Queue.is_empty t.hostout)) lsl 4)
   233	  lor (Bool.to_int (not (Queue.is_empty t.smp.fifo)) lsl 5)
   234	  lor (Bool.to_int (SM.full t.str) lsl 6) lor (Bool.to_int t.act_drop lsl 7)
   235	
   236	let read_reg t a =
   237	  if a >= reg_space then 0
   238	  else if rw_bits a > 0 then t.regs.(a)
   239	  else if a = r_status then status t
   240	  else if a = r_hostin_count then Queue.length t.hostin
   241	  else if a = r_hostout_count then Queue.length t.hostout
   242	  else if a >= r_crc_raw && a < r_crc_raw + 4 then (t.crc lsr (8 * (a - r_crc_raw))) land 0xFF
   243	  else if a = r_match then (let y, hit = matcher_now t in y lor (Bool.to_int hit lsl 7))
   244	  else if a = r_smp_overflows then t.smp.overflows land 0xFF
   245	  else 0
   246	
   247	(* the byte a fetch returns, and whether it pops the sampler *)
   248	let fetch t (tgt, a) =
   249	  let a = a land 0xFFFF in
   250	  if tgt = t_reg then (read_reg t a, false)

     1	(* The board around the chip core: the four-phase stage's timing at the pads (README, "Phases"),
     2	   the host on the host link, and the outside world on the general pads.
     3	
     4	   Clock k: the pads show the core's outputs of clock k - 1 (the stage registers them); the outside
     5	   world and the host react to those levels; the core sees in clock k the samples taken in clock
     6	   k - 2. Levels are nibbles: bit p is the level during quarter p. *)
     7	
     8	open Regs
     9	
    10	type core = { step : smp:int array -> reset:bool -> Chip_spec.outputs }
    11	
    12	let spec_core (s : Chip_spec.t) = { step = (fun ~smp ~reset -> Chip_spec.step s ~smp ~reset) }
    13	let rtl_core (r : Chip_sim.t) = { step = (fun ~smp ~reset -> Chip_sim.step r ~smp ~reset) }
    14	
    15	let zero_out : Chip_spec.outputs = { pad_nib = Array.make n_pads 0; uio_oe = 0 }
    16	
    17	type t = {
    18	  mutable host : Host.t;
    19	  mutable out : Chip_spec.outputs;     (* what the pads show during this clock *)
    20	  mutable pipe : int array list;       (* the last two clocks' samples, oldest first *)
    21	  mutable clock : int;
    22	  mutable trace : (int array * int) list;  (* optional: per clock, pad levels and uio_oe *)
    23	  keep_trace : bool;
    24	}
    25	
    26	let create ?(keep_trace = false) () =
    27	  { host = Host.create (); out = zero_out; pipe = [ Array.make n_pads 0; Array.make n_pads 0 ]; clock = 0;
    28	    trace = []; keep_trace }
    29	
    30	let rep4 b = if b <> 0 then 15 else 0
    31	let bit v i = (v lsr i) land 1
    32	
    33	(* The pad levels of the uio pins during this clock: the chip's where it drives, else [ext]. *)
    34	let resolve_uio (out : Chip_spec.outputs) i ~ext = if bit out.uio_oe (i - 8) = 1 then out.pad_nib.(i) else ext
    35	
    36	(* The samples of this clock, given the outside world's levels [ext] (16 nibbles; entries for
    37	   the host-link pads are ignored) and the host. Advances the host by one clock. *)
    38	let samples b ~ext =
    39	  let out = b.out in
    40	  let d_chip k = if bit out.uio_oe k = 1 then Some (bit out.pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k) 3) else None in
    41	  let d_in = ref 0 in
    42	  for k = 0 to 3 do
    43	    let v = match d_chip k with Some v -> v | None -> (match b.host.d with Some d -> bit d k | None -> 0) in
    44	    d_in := !d_in lor (v lsl k)
    45	  done;
    46	  let s, r, d = Host.clock b.host ~d_in:!d_in in
    47	  let smp = Array.copy ext in
    48	  for k = 0 to 3 do
    49	    smp.(pad_hdata + k) <-
    50	      (if bit out.uio_oe k = 1 then out.pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k)
    51	       else match d with Some d -> rep4 (bit d k) | None -> 0)
    52	  done;
    53	  smp.(pad_hstb) <- rep4 s;
    54	  smp.(pad_hrd) <- rep4 r;
    55	  smp
    56	
    57	(* One clock with the samples [smp] taken in it: the core sees the samples of two clocks ago. *)
    58	let advance b (cores : core list) ~smp ~reset =
    59	  let seen = List.hd b.pipe in
    60	  b.pipe <- List.tl b.pipe @ [ smp ];
    61	  let outs = List.map (fun c -> c.step ~smp:seen ~reset) cores in
    62	  if b.keep_trace then b.trace <- (Array.copy b.out.pad_nib, b.out.uio_oe) :: b.trace;
    63	  b.out <- List.hd outs;
    64	  b.clock <- b.clock + 1;
    65	  outs

     1	(* Lockstep of the chip core's RTL against its executable specification, every clock, on random
     2	   programmes, random pad inputs and random host-link traffic; then the planted integration bugs,
     3	   each of which must be caught.
     4	
     5	     lockstep.exe run TRIALS CLOCKS [SEED]      the design
     6	     lockstep.exe controls TRIALS CLOCKS        every planted bug
     7	     lockstep.exe layouts TRIALS CLOCKS         the design at 4, 8 and 16 PEs
     8	
     9	   Compared every clock: every output pad's nibble and the uio output enables (what the pins
    10	   show); and the architectural state of the sequencer and of every PE, the host FIFOs' counts,
    11	   the CRC register, the NCO's output register, the matcher's sum, the edge sampler's outputs
    12	   and the host link's read buffer. *)

    19	(* ---- random programmes ---- *)
    20	
    21	(* Generators, chosen per trial (seed mod the number of modes), each biased towards the paths
    22	   that uniform traffic reaches rarely:
    23	   - mixed: everything;
    24	   - ports: threads SEND and RECV on the ports while the host writes the same segments' feeds;
    25	   - host: threads OUT and IN while the host polls HOSTOUT and fills HOSTIN;
    26	   - memory: the threads' first words are memory and FIFO instructions (fetched again on every
    27	     clock in clear), and the host reads and writes the bank and the store while stopped;
    28	   - race: rare OUTs while the host polls HOSTOUT without pause, so that an entry arrives
    29	     between the two bytes of a read. *)
    30	let modes = [| "mixed"; "ports"; "host"; "memory"; "race" |]
    31	
    32	let gen_programme ?(mode = "mixed") r ~words ~base =
    33	  let i n = Random.State.int r n and chance p = Random.State.float r 1.0 < p in
    34	  let a () = base + i words in
    35	  let pick l = List.nth l (i (List.length l)) in
    36	  Array.init words (fun k ->
    37	      let x = i 100 in
    38	      let x =
    39	        match mode with
    40	        | "ports" -> if chance 0.5 then 53 + i 16 else x
    41	        | "host" -> if chance 0.4 then (if i 3 = 0 then 50 else 45 + i 5) else x
    42	        | "memory" -> if k = 0 then List.nth [ 87; 87; 83; 50; 64 ] (i 5) else x
    43	        | "race" -> if x >= 45 && x < 50 && i 4 > 0 then 37 else if x < 28 && x >= 23 then 24 else x
    44	        | _ -> x

    46	      if x < 9 then Isa2.setp ~q:(i 4) ~mask:(i 256) ~value:(i 2) ~oe:(i 2) ()

    48	      else if x < 19 then Isa2.shi ~quad:(i 2) ~pin:(i 8) ~msb:(i 2) ()
    49	      else if x < 23 then Isa2.ldc (i 20)
    50	      else if x < 28 then Isa2.ldd (if chance 0.7 then 0 else i 30)
    51	      else if x < 33 then Isa2.lda (if chance 0.5 then i 8 else i 256)
    52	      else if x < 36 then Isa2.waitp ~pin:(i 8) ~value:(i 2) ~fail:(a ())
    53	      else if x < 38 then Isa2.waitd
    54	      else if x < 42 then Isa2.jmp (a ())
    55	      else if x < 45 then Isa2.jnz (a ())
    56	      else if x < 50 then (if chance 0.5 then Isa2.out ~tag:(i 8) () else Isa2.outi ~tag:(i 8) (i 256))
    57	      else if x < 53 then Isa2.in_
    58	      else if x < 62 then Isa2.send ~ch:(pick [ 4; 5; 6; 7; 4; 5; 0; 1; 2; 3 ]) ~fail:(a ())
    59	      else if x < 69 then Isa2.recv ~ch:(pick [ 4; 5; 6; 7; 4; 5; 0; 1; 2; 3 ]) ~fail:(a ())
    60	      else if x < 76 then Isa2.waitc ~cond:(pick [ 12; 13; 14; 15; 12; 13; 9; 10; 11; i 16 ]) ~fail:(a ())

    62	      else if x < 81 then Isa2.skeq (i 4)

    64	      else if x < 87 then Isa2.ldb

    87	let state_diffs (s : S.t) (r : Chip_sim.t) =
    88	  let d = ref [] in
    89	  let cmp name a b = if a <> b then d := Printf.sprintf "%s: spec %x rtl %x" name a b :: !d in
    90	  let per name w = Array.init 4 (fun t -> (Chip_sim.get r ("seq_dbg_" ^ name) lsr (w * t)) land ((1 lsl w) - 1)) in
    91	  let pend = Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_pend" in
    92	  let st = s.seq in
    93	  let cmp4 name w (a : int array) =
    94	    let b = per name w in
    95	    Array.iteri (fun t x -> if not (name = "acc" && pend land 4 <> 0 && pend land 3 = t) then cmp (Printf.sprintf "seq %s%d" name t) x b.(t)) a
    96	  in
    97	  cmp4 "pc" 8 st.pcs; cmp4 "page" 2 st.pages; cmp4 "acc" 8 st.accs; cmp4 "cnt" 12 st.cnts; cmp4 "dl" 12 st.dls;
    98	  cmp4 "bp" 10 st.bps; cmp4 "fine" 8 st.fines; cmp4 "armed" 1 st.armed; cmp4 "cfg" 8 st.cfgs;
    99	  cmp4 "lsend" 3 st.lsend; cmp4 "inbox" 8 st.inbox; cmp4 "full" 1 st.full;
   100	  cmp "seq thread" st.thread (Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_thread");
   101	  cmp "seq latch" st.latch (Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_latch");
   102	  let pes = (Upe.Model.state s.pe).pes in
   103	  Array.iteri

   109	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d L" i) (Bool.to_int p.l) (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_l%d" i));

   120	    (Upe.Model.state s.pe).segs;
   121	  cmp "send byte order" (Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.mapi (fun p b -> Bool.to_int b lsl p) s.send_hi)) (Chip_sim.get r "send_hi");
   122	  let holds = Chip_sim.get_bits r "holds" in

   127	  done;

   146	(* the host's random traffic: a setup, then transactions while the programme runs *)
   147	let setup_traffic ?(mode = "mixed") r (h : Host.t) ~(cfg : S.config) =
   148	  let i n = Random.State.int r n and chance p = Random.State.float r 1.0 < p in
   149	  let w = Host.wreg h in
   150	  let block a l = Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_reg; addr = a; data = l }) in
   151	  w r_ctrl 2;                                   (* stopped, assists held *)
   152	  (* the programme: each thread a region of a page *)
   153	  let pages = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> i 4) and bases = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> i 224) in
   154	  for t = 0 to 3 do
   155	    let words = gen_programme ~mode r ~words:(8 + i 24) ~base:bases.(t) in
   156	    let addr = ((pages.(t) lsl 8) lor bases.(t)) land (cfg.prog_words - 1) in
   157	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = 2 * addr;
   158	                                data = List.concat_map (fun w -> [ w land 0xFF; w lsr 8 ]) (Array.to_list words) })
   159	  done;
   160	  block (r_boot_pc 0) (Array.to_list bases @ [ Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.mapi (fun t p -> p lsl (2 * t)) pages) ]);
   161	  (* pads, pins, sampler pins, flags *)
   162	  block (r_padsel 0)
   163	    (List.init 16 (fun _ -> i 128) @ List.init 8 (fun _ -> i 16) @ List.init 4 (fun _ -> i 16) @ [ i 2 ]);
   164	  block (r_flagsel 0) (List.init 16 (fun _ -> if chance 0.8 then i 17 else i 32));
   165	  (* the assists, 0x40..0x6F *)
   166	  let width () = List.nth [ 1; 2; 4 ] (i 3) in
   167	  let width_crc = List.nth [ 5; 8; 15; 16; 32 ] (i 5) in
   168	  let mask = if width_crc = 32 then 0xFFFF_FFFF else (1 lsl width_crc) - 1 in
   169	  let le32 v = List.init 4 (fun k -> (v lsr (8 * k)) land 0xFF) in
   170	  let rb () = Random.State.bits r in
   171	  block r_str_per
   172	    ([ 1 + i 6; width () lsl 4; i 256; i 16 ]                                    (* 0x40 streamer *)
   173	     @ [ 1 + i 6; (width () lsl 4) lor (i 2 lsl 7); i 6; (i 4 lsl 4) lor (i 2 lsl 6); i 12 ] (* 0x44 sampler *)
   174	     @ [ 0; 0; 0 ]                                                               (* 0x49-0x4B *)
   175	     @ [ i 16; i 256; 4 + i 12; 0; 8 + i 60; i 2; 4 + i 8; 4 + i 12 ]           (* 0x4C edge sampler *)
   176	     @ [ i 4; 0; 0; 0 ]                                                          (* 0x54 CRC control *)
   177	     @ le32 (rb () land mask) @ le32 (rb () land mask) @ le32 mask @ le32 (rb ())
   178	     @ le32 (if chance 0.5 then 0 else rb () land mask)
   179	     @ le32 (rb () lor (rb () lsl 30)));                                         (* 0x6C NCO *)
   180	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_match; addr = 0; data = List.init 37 (fun _ -> i 2) });
   181	  (* the array: configuration and init chains, segment registers *)
   182	  let l = cfg.layout in
   183	  for sg = 0 to 3 do
   184	    let n = l.end_.(sg) - l.start.(sg) + 1 in
   185	    let bytes = List.concat (List.init n (fun _ -> List.rev (Array.to_list (Upe.Spec.bytes_of_op (rand_op r))))) in
   186	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_pecfg; addr = sg lsl 8; data = bytes });
   187	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peinit; addr = sg lsl 8; data = List.init (2 * n) (fun _ -> i 256) });
   188	    let src = if mode = "ports" then List.nth [ 2; 2; 2; 0 ] (i 4) else List.nth [ 0; 0; 1; 2; 2; 3; 5 ] (i 7) in
   189	    let ctrl = src lor (i 2 lsl 3) lor (if chance 0.85 then 16 else 0) lor (i 2 lsl 5) lor (i 2 lsl 6) in
   190	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (sg lsl 2) lor 2; data = [ ctrl ] });
   191	    if chance 0.4 then Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (sg lsl 2) lor 3; data = [ List.nth [ 1; 2; 3; 5; 9 ] (i 5) ] })
   192	  done;
   193	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_bank; addr = (if mode = "memory" then 0 else i 1024); data = List.init 16 (fun _ -> i 256) });
   194	  w r_ctrl 0;                                    (* release the assists *)
   195	  w r_ctrl 1                                     (* run *)
   196	
   197	let running_traffic ?(mode = "mixed") r (h : Host.t) =
   198	  let i n = Random.State.int r n in
   199	  let w = Host.wreg h in
   200	  let ignore_k _ = () in
   201	  let x = i 22 in
   202	  let x =
   203	    match mode with
   204	    | "ports" -> if i 2 = 0 then List.nth [ 9; 10; 13 ] (i 3) else x
   205	    | "host" -> if i 2 = 0 then List.nth [ 0; 3; 4; 3 ] (i 4) else x
   206	    | "memory" -> if i 2 = 0 then 18 else x
   207	    | "race" -> if i 4 > 0 then 3 else x
   208	    | _ -> x
   209	  in
   210	  match x with
   211	  | 0 | 1 | 2 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_hostin; addr = 0; data = List.init (1 + i 4) (fun _ -> i 256) })
   212	  | 3 | 4 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_hostout; addr = 0; n = 2 * (1 + i 3); k = ignore_k })
   213	  | 5 | 6 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = i reg_space; n = 1 + i 4; k = ignore_k })
   214	  | 7 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_stream; addr = 0; data = [ i 256; i 256; i 16 ] })
   215	  | 8 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_sample; addr = 0; n = 4; k = ignore_k })
   216	  | 9 | 10 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (i 4 lsl 2) lor (List.nth [ 0; 1; 1; 2; 3 ] (i 5)); data = [ i 256 ] })
   217	  | 11 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = (if i 2 = 0 then t_pecfg else t_peinit); addr = i 4 lsl 8; data = [ i 256 ] })
   218	  | 12 -> w r_restart (i 16); w r_restart_pc (i 256)
   219	  | 13 -> w r_port_reset (i 16)
   220	  | 14 -> w r_crc_start 0
   221	  | 15 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_status; n = 1; k = ignore_k }); w r_status 0
   222	  | 16 -> Host.wreg32 h r_nco_inc (Random.State.bits r)
   223	  | 17 -> w (r_flagsel (i 16)) (i 32); w (r_padsel (List.nth general_out_pads (i 10))) (i 128)
   224	  | 18 ->
   225	    (* stop, touch the memories, start again *)
   226	    w r_ctrl 0;
   227	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = 2 * i 1024; data = [ i 256; i 256 ] });
   228	    Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_prog; addr = i 2048; n = 2; k = ignore_k });
   229	    let ba () = if i 2 = 0 || mode = "memory" then i 4 else i 1024 in
   230	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_bank; addr = ba (); data = [ i 256 ] });
   231	    Host.submit h (Host.Idle (i 30));
   232	    Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_bank; addr = ba (); n = 2; k = ignore_k });
   233	    w r_ctrl 1
   234	  | 19 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = i 2048; data = [ i 256 ] })   (* refused while running *)
   235	  | 20 ->
   236	    (* hold the assists, change their configuration, release *)
   237	    w r_ctrl 3; w r_es_mode (i 16); w r_smp_per (1 + i 5);
   238	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_match; addr = 0; data = [ i 2; i 2; i 2 ] }); w r_ctrl 1
   239	  | _ -> Host.submit h (Host.Idle (i 40))
   240	
   241	(* the outside world: each general input pad flips with a per-trial rate per quarter *)
   242	let ext_world r ~rates =
   243	  let lv = Array.make n_pads 0 in
   244	  fun (out : S.outputs) ->
   245	    let smp = Array.make n_pads 0 in
   246	    List.iter
   247	      (fun p ->
   248	        let n = ref 0 in
   249	        for q = 0 to 3 do
   250	          if Random.State.float r 1.0 < rates.(p) then lv.(p) <- 1 - lv.(p);
   251	          n := !n lor (lv.(p) lsl q)
   252	        done;
   253	        smp.(p) <- !n)
   254	      [ 0; 1; 2; 3; 4; 5; 6; 7; 14; 15 ];
   255	    (* a uio pad the chip drives reads back what it drives *)
   256	    List.iter (fun p -> smp.(p) <- Board.resolve_uio out p ~ext:smp.(p)) [ 14; 15 ];
   257	    smp
   258	
   259	exception Mismatch of int * string list
   260	
   261	(* Runs one trial; returns the clock of the first mismatch and the differences, or None. *)
   262	let run_trial ?(compare_state = true) { cfg; clocks; seed } =
   263	  let r = Random.State.make [| seed |] in
   264	  let r_gen = r in
   265	  let mode = modes.(seed mod Array.length modes) in
   266	  let spec = S.create ~cfg () and rtl = Chip_sim.create ~cfg () in
   267	  let cores = [ Board.spec_core spec; Board.rtl_core rtl ] in
   268	  let b = Board.create () in
   269	  let rates = Array.init n_pads (fun _ -> List.nth [ 0.0; 0.01; 0.05; 0.2 ] (Random.State.int r 4)) in
   270	  let world = ext_world r ~rates in
   271	  setup_traffic ~mode r b.host ~cfg;
   272	  let reset_at = if Random.State.int r 4 = 0 then Random.State.int r clocks else -1 in
   273	  try
   274	    for c = 0 to clocks - 1 do
   275	      if Host.idle b.host then running_traffic ~mode r b.host;
   276	      let smp = Board.samples b ~ext:(world b.out) in
   277	      let reset = c < 2 || (c >= reset_at && c < reset_at + 3) in
   278	      (* a reset in the middle of a host transaction desynchronises the link: the host starts
   279	         again, as a real one would after resetting the chip *)
   280	      if c = reset_at then begin
   281	        (* the host abandons its transaction when it resets the chip, and waits until the
   282	           synchronisers have settled before it starts again *)
   283	        let s = b.host.s in
   284	        b.host <- Host.create ();
   285	        b.host.s <- s;
   286	        Host.submit b.host (Host.Idle 16);
   287	        setup_traffic ~mode r_gen b.host ~cfg
   288	      end;
   289	      (match Board.advance b cores ~smp ~reset with
   290	       | [ a; o ] ->
   291	         let d = out_diffs a o in
   292	         let d = if d = [] && compare_state then state_diffs spec rtl else d in
   293	         let d = if d = [] then d else
   294	             d @ [ Printf.sprintf "(sampler: cfg clocked %b period %d width %d trig %d/%d offset %d frame %d; fifo %s; held %b)"
   295	                     spec.smp.cfg.clocked spec.smp.cfg.period spec.smp.cfg.width spec.smp.cfg.trig_pin spec.smp.cfg.trig_val
   296	                     spec.smp.cfg.offset spec.smp.cfg.frame_len
   297	                     (String.concat "," (List.map (fun (w, n) -> Printf.sprintf "%04x/%d" w n) (List.of_seq (Queue.to_seq spec.smp.fifo))))
   298	                     (S.held spec) ]
   299	               @ [ (match spec.last_fetch with Some (tg, a, b) -> Printf.sprintf "(last fetch: target %d address %x byte %x)" tg a b | None -> "(no fetch yet)") ] in
   300	         if d <> [] then raise (Mismatch (c, d))
   301	       | _ -> assert false)
   302	    done;
   303	    None
   304	  with Mismatch (c, d) -> Some (c, d)
   305	
   306	let campaign ?(stop = true) ~cfg ~trials ~clocks ~seed0 () =
   307	  let fails = ref [] and total = ref 0 in
   308	  (try
   309	     for k = 0 to trials - 1 do
   310	       match (try run_trial { cfg; clocks; seed = seed0 + k } with e -> Some (-1, [ "exception: " ^ Printexc.to_string e ])) with
   311	       | None -> total := !total + clocks
   312	       | Some (c, d) ->
   313	         total := !total + c + 1;
   314	         fails := (seed0 + k, c, d) :: !fails;
   315	         if stop then raise Exit
   316	     done
   317	   with Exit -> ());
   318	  (!total, List.rev !fails)
   319	
   320	let layout_name (l : Upe.Spec.layout) =
   321	  String.concat "|" (List.init 4 (fun j -> string_of_int (l.end_.(j) - l.start.(j) + 1)))
   322	
   323	let run_design ~cfg ~trials ~clocks ~seed0 =
   324	  Chip_rtl.bug := "";
   325	  let t0 = Unix.gettimeofday () in
   326	  let total, fails = campaign ~stop:false ~cfg ~trials ~clocks ~seed0 () in
   327	  pr "layout %s, store %d words: %d trials x %d clocks, %d clocks compared, %d trials with mismatches (%.0f s)\n"
   328	    (layout_name cfg.layout) cfg.prog_words trials clocks total (List.length fails) (Unix.gettimeofday () -. t0);
   329	  List.iter (fun (s, c, d) -> pr "  seed %d, clock %d:\n    %s\n" s c (String.concat "\n    " (List.filteri (fun i _ -> i < 8) d))) fails;
   330	  let cov = List.sort compare (Hashtbl.fold (fun k v acc -> (k, v) :: acc) S.cov []) in
   331	  pr "  coverage (events counted on the specification): %s\n"
   332	    (String.concat ", " (List.map (fun (k, v) -> Printf.sprintf "%s %d" k v) cov));
   333	  Hashtbl.reset S.cov;
   334	  flush stdout;
   335	  fails = []
   336	
   337	let controls ~cfg ~trials ~clocks =
   338	  let caught = ref 0 in
   339	  List.iter
   340	    (fun (b, what) ->
   341	      Chip_rtl.bug := b;
   342	      let _, fails = campaign ~cfg ~trials ~clocks ~seed0:5000 () in
   343	      (match fails with
   344	       | (s, c, d) :: _ ->
   345	         incr caught;
   346	         pr "%-20s caught: seed %d clock %5d  (%s)\n    first diff: %s\n" b s c what (List.hd d)
   347	       | [] -> pr "%-20s MISSED in %d trials  (%s)\n" b trials what);
   348	      flush stdout)
   349	    Chip_rtl.bugs;
   350	  Chip_rtl.bug := "";
   351	  pr "%d of %d planted integration bugs caught\n" !caught (List.length Chip_rtl.bugs)
   352	
   353	let () =
   354	  let cfg = S.default_config in
   355	  match Array.to_list Sys.argv with
   356	  | [ _; "run"; n; c ] -> exit (if run_design ~cfg ~trials:(int_of_string n) ~clocks:(int_of_string c) ~seed0:1 then 0 else 1)
   357	  | [ _; "run"; n; c; s ] ->
   358	    exit (if run_design ~cfg ~trials:(int_of_string n) ~clocks:(int_of_string c) ~seed0:(int_of_string s) then 0 else 1)
   359	  | [ _; "controls"; n; c ] -> controls ~cfg ~trials:(int_of_string n) ~clocks:(int_of_string c)
   360	  | [ _; "layouts"; n; c ] ->
   361	    List.iter
   362	      (fun sizes ->
   363	        ignore (run_design ~cfg:{ cfg with layout = Upe.Spec.layout_of_sizes sizes } ~trials:(int_of_string n)
   364	                  ~clocks:(int_of_string c) ~seed0:100))
   365	      [ [| 1; 1; 1; 1 |]; [| 2; 2; 2; 2 |]; [| 2; 2; 4; 8 |] ];
   366	    ignore (run_design ~cfg:{ cfg with prog_words = 256 } ~trials:(int_of_string n) ~clocks:(int_of_string c) ~seed0:200)
   367	  | _ -> prerr_endline "usage: lockstep.exe run N CLOCKS [SEED] | controls N CLOCKS | layouts N CLOCKS"

     1	(* The host's side of the host link (README, "Host link protocol"), as a per-clock driver: it
     2	   decides what the host drives on S, R and D in each clock and samples D when it reads. It
     3	   obeys the protocol's timing with the margins below, and knows nothing about the chip beyond
     4	   the protocol. *)
     5	
     6	open Regs
     7	
     8	type txn =
     9	  | Write of { tgt : int; addr : int; data : int list }
    10	  | Read of { tgt : int; addr : int; n : int; k : int list -> unit }   (* n >= 1 bytes *)
    11	  | Idle of int
    12	  | Call of (unit -> unit)                                              (* runs when reached *)
    13	
    14	(* micro-steps, one or more per clock until a wait *)
    15	type step =
    16	  | Set_d of int                (* drive D *)
    17	  | Release_d
    18	  | Toggle_s
    19	  | Set_r of int
    20	  | Wait of int                 (* clocks *)
    21	  | Sample of (int -> unit)     (* read D now *)
    22	  | Do of (unit -> unit)
    23	
    24	let write_setup = 2 and write_hold = 2 and read_gap = 8 and turnaround = 8
    25	
    26	type t = {
    27	  q : step Queue.t;
    28	  mutable wait : int;
    29	  mutable s : int; mutable r : int; mutable d : int option;
    30	  mutable busy_clocks : int;
    31	}
    32	
    33	let create () = { q = Queue.create (); wait = 0; s = 0; r = 0; d = None; busy_clocks = 0 }
    34	
    35	let idle t = Queue.is_empty t.q && t.wait = 0
    36	
    37	let push_steps t l = List.iter (fun s -> Queue.push s t.q) l
    38	
    39	let byte_steps b =
    40	  [ Set_d (b lsr 4); Wait write_setup; Toggle_s; Wait write_hold;
    41	    Set_d (b land 15); Wait write_setup; Toggle_s; Wait write_hold ]
    42	
    43	let cmd t ~op ~tgt ~addr ~count =
    44	  List.iter (fun b -> push_steps t (byte_steps b))
    45	    [ (op lsl 4) lor tgt; (addr lsr 8) land 0xFF; addr land 0xFF; count - 1 ]
    46	
    47	let submit t = function
    48	  | Write { tgt; addr; data } ->
    49	    (* at most 256 bytes per command *)
    50	    let rec go addr = function
    51	      | [] -> ()
    52	      | l ->
    53	        let chunk = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i < 256) l and rest = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i >= 256) l in
    54	        cmd t ~op:op_write ~tgt ~addr ~count:(List.length chunk);
    55	        List.iter (fun b -> push_steps t (byte_steps b)) chunk;
    56	        go (addr + List.length chunk) rest
    57	    in
    58	    go addr data
    59	  | Read { tgt; addr; n; k } ->
    60	    assert (n >= 1 && n <= 256);
    61	    cmd t ~op:op_read ~tgt ~addr ~count:n;
    62	    let acc = ref [] and hi = ref 0 in
    63	    push_steps t [ Release_d; Wait turnaround; Set_r 1; Wait read_gap ];
    64	    for _ = 1 to n do
    65	      push_steps t
    66	        [ Sample (fun v -> hi := v); Toggle_s; Wait read_gap;
    67	          Sample (fun v -> acc := ((!hi lsl 4) lor v) :: !acc); Toggle_s; Wait read_gap ]
    68	    done;
    69	    push_steps t [ Set_r 0; Wait turnaround; Do (fun () -> k (List.rev !acc)) ]
    70	  | Idle n -> push_steps t [ Wait n ]
    71	  | Call f -> push_steps t [ Do f ]
    72	
    73	(* One clock. [d_in] is the level of the four data lines during this clock (whoever drives
    74	   them). Returns what the host drives during this clock: (S, R, D or None). *)
    75	let clock t ~d_in =
    76	  if t.wait > 0 then t.wait <- t.wait - 1
    77	  else begin
    78	    let continue = ref true in
    79	    while !continue && not (Queue.is_empty t.q) do
    80	      match Queue.pop t.q with
    81	      | Set_d v -> t.d <- Some v
    82	      | Release_d -> t.d <- None
    83	      | Toggle_s -> t.s <- 1 - t.s
    84	      | Set_r v -> t.r <- v
    85	      | Wait n -> t.wait <- n - 1; continue := false
    86	      | Sample f -> f d_in
    87	      | Do f -> f ()
    88	    done
    89	  end;
    90	  if not (idle t) then t.busy_clocks <- t.busy_clocks + 1;
    91	  (t.s, t.r, t.d)
    92	
    93	(* convenience: register writes and reads *)
    94	let wreg t a v = submit t (Write { tgt = t_reg; addr = a; data = [ v land 0xFF ] })
    95	let wreg32 t a v = submit t (Write { tgt = t_reg; addr = a; data = List.init 4 (fun k -> (v lsr (8 * k)) land 0xFF) })

   438	         quarter points, registered; then the accumulator advances by inc *)
   439	      let inc = reg32 t r_nco_inc in
   440	      let a = [| t.nco_acc; u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 2)); u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 1));
   441	                 u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 1) + (inc lsr 2)) |] in
   442	      let l p = 1 - bit a.(p) 31 in
   443	      t.nco_out <- (l 0 lor (l 1 lsl 1) lor (l 2 lsl 2) lor (l 3 lsl 3))
   444	                   lor ((l 0 lor (l 0 lsl 1) lor (l 2 lsl 2) lor (l 2 lsl 3)) lsl 4)
   445	                   lor (rep4 (l 0) lsl 8);
   446	      t.nco_acc <- u32 (t.nco_acc + inc)
   447	    end;
   448	    (match act with
   449	     | Some (A_match b) -> for k = 36 downto 1 do t.chain.(k) <- t.chain.(k - 1) done; t.chain.(0) <- b
   450	     | _ -> ());
   451	    (* memories *)
   452	    (match act with
   453	     | Some (A_prog (a, b)) ->
   454	       if running then t.err_mem <- true
   455	       else if a land 1 = 0 then t.prog_lo <- b
   456	       else t.prog.((a lsr 1) land (t.cfg.prog_words - 1)) <- (b lsl 8) lor t.prog_lo
   457	     | Some (A_bank (a, b)) -> if running then t.err_mem <- true else t.bank.(a land (bank_bytes - 1)) <- b
   458	     | Some (A_stream (0, b)) -> t.str_lo <- b
   459	     | Some (A_stream (1, b)) -> t.str_hi <- b
   460	     | _ -> ());
   461	    (* the host link *)
   462	    (match fetched with Some (b, _) -> t.rbuf <- b | None -> ());
   463	    t.pend_fetch <- None;
   464	    let next_action = ref None in
   465	    let s = lvl pad_hstb and r = lvl pad_hrd in
   466	    let d = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 4 (fun k -> lvl (pad_hdata + k) lsl k)) in
   467	    if r <> t.prev_r then begin
   468	      (* raising R starts reading what a READ command fetched; lowering it ends the command *)
   469	      t.nphase <- 0; t.rphase <- 0;
   470	      if r = 0 then t.pst <- Idle
   471	    end
   472	    else if s <> t.prev_s then begin
   473	      if r = 0 then begin
   474	        if t.nphase = 0 then (t.hi_nib <- d; t.nphase <- 1)
   475	        else begin
   476	          t.nphase <- 0;
   477	          let b = (t.hi_nib lsl 4) lor d in
   478	          match t.pst with
   479	          | Idle ->
   480	            let op = b lsr 4 in
   481	            if op = op_write || op = op_read then (t.op <- op; t.tgt <- b land 15; t.pst <- Addr_hi)
   482	          | Addr_hi -> t.addr <- b lsl 8; t.pst <- Addr_lo
   483	          | Addr_lo -> t.addr <- t.addr lor b; t.pst <- Count
   484	          | Count ->
   485	            t.remaining <- b + 1;
   486	            if t.op = op_write then t.pst <- Wdata
   487	            else (t.pst <- Rdata; t.pend_fetch <- Some (t.tgt, t.addr))
   488	          | Wdata ->
   489	            next_action := action_of ~tgt:t.tgt ~addr:t.addr b;
   490	            t.addr <- (t.addr + 1) land 0xFFFF;
   491	            t.remaining <- t.remaining - 1;
   492	            if t.remaining = 0 then t.pst <- Idle
   493	          | Rdata -> t.pst <- Idle
   494	        end
   495	      end
   496	      else if t.rphase = 0 then t.rphase <- 1
   497	      else begin
   498	        t.rphase <- 0;
   499	        if t.pst = Rdata && t.remaining > 1 then begin
   500	          t.remaining <- t.remaining - 1;
   501	          t.addr <- (t.addr + 1) land 0xFFFF;
   502	          t.pend_fetch <- Some (t.tgt, t.addr)
   503	        end
   504	        else begin
   505	          t.remaining <- 0; t.rbuf <- 0;
   506	          if t.pst = Rdata then t.pst <- Idle
   507	        end
   508	      end
   509	    end;
   510	    t.prev_s <- s; t.prev_r <- r;
   511	    (* an action waits a clock when a thread's SEND took the array's write port; a newer byte
   512	       overwrites a waiting one, and that is recorded *)
   513	    let waiting = match act with Some (A_peseg _) when not host_mbx_done -> hit "host feed write waited for a SEND"; act | _ -> None in
   514	    (match !next_action, waiting with
   515	     | Some a, Some _ -> t.act_drop <- true; t.pend_action <- Some a
   516	     | Some a, None -> t.pend_action <- Some a
   517	     | None, w -> t.pend_action <- w);
   518	    (* register writes land at the end of the clock *)
   519	    (match act with
   520	     | Some (A_reg (a, b)) ->
   521	       if a = r_status then (t.err_mem <- false; t.hin_ovf <- false; t.hout_ovf <- false; t.act_drop <- false)
   522	       else if a < reg_space && rw_bits a > 0 then t.regs.(a) <- b land ((1 lsl rw_bits a) - 1)
   523	     | _ -> ());
   524	    (* a clock spent in clear leaves the sequencer in its reset state, from the boot registers as
   525	       they were during the clock; a held streamer and sampler are recreated with the
   526	       configuration as it is now, which is what their idle outputs show in the next clock *)
   527	    if not running then t.seq <- Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:boot_now ~bank:t.bank;
   528	    if hold then begin
   529	      t.str <- SM.create (streamer_cfg t);
   530	      t.smp <- PM.create (sampler_cfg t)
   531	    end;
   532	    out
   533	  end

codex
The README describes this as a prototype integration, but its “How it is checked” section makes broad claims about per-clock output comparison and independent judges. I’m now checking those claims against the actual harness mechanics and the reset/host transaction boundaries, where model agreement can mask a shared contract error.

     1	(* The board around the chip core: the four-phase stage's timing at the pads (README, "Phases"),
     2	   the host on the host link, and the outside world on the general pads.
     3	
     4	   Clock k: the pads show the core's outputs of clock k - 1 (the stage registers them); the outside
     5	   world and the host react to those levels; the core sees in clock k the samples taken in clock
     6	   k - 2. Levels are nibbles: bit p is the level during quarter p. *)
     7	
     8	open Regs
     9	
    10	type core = { step : smp:int array -> reset:bool -> Chip_spec.outputs }
    11	
    12	let spec_core (s : Chip_spec.t) = { step = (fun ~smp ~reset -> Chip_spec.step s ~smp ~reset) }
    13	let rtl_core (r : Chip_sim.t) = { step = (fun ~smp ~reset -> Chip_sim.step r ~smp ~reset) }
    14	
    15	let zero_out : Chip_spec.outputs = { pad_nib = Array.make n_pads 0; uio_oe = 0 }
    16	
    17	type t = {
    18	  mutable host : Host.t;
    19	  mutable out : Chip_spec.outputs;     (* what the pads show during this clock *)
    20	  mutable pipe : int array list;       (* the last two clocks' samples, oldest first *)
    21	  mutable clock : int;
    22	  mutable trace : (int array * int) list;  (* optional: per clock, pad levels and uio_oe *)
    23	  keep_trace : bool;
    24	}
    25	
    26	let create ?(keep_trace = false) () =
    27	  { host = Host.create (); out = zero_out; pipe = [ Array.make n_pads 0; Array.make n_pads 0 ]; clock = 0;
    28	    trace = []; keep_trace }
    29	
    30	let rep4 b = if b <> 0 then 15 else 0
    31	let bit v i = (v lsr i) land 1
    32	
    33	(* The pad levels of the uio pins during this clock: the chip's where it drives, else [ext]. *)
    34	let resolve_uio (out : Chip_spec.outputs) i ~ext = if bit out.uio_oe (i - 8) = 1 then out.pad_nib.(i) else ext
    35	
    36	(* The samples of this clock, given the outside world's levels [ext] (16 nibbles; entries for
    37	   the host-link pads are ignored) and the host. Advances the host by one clock. *)
    38	let samples b ~ext =
    39	  let out = b.out in
    40	  let d_chip k = if bit out.uio_oe k = 1 then Some (bit out.pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k) 3) else None in
    41	  let d_in = ref 0 in
    42	  for k = 0 to 3 do
    43	    let v = match d_chip k with Some v -> v | None -> (match b.host.d with Some d -> bit d k | None -> 0) in
    44	    d_in := !d_in lor (v lsl k)
    45	  done;
    46	  let s, r, d = Host.clock b.host ~d_in:!d_in in
    47	  let smp = Array.copy ext in
    48	  for k = 0 to 3 do
    49	    smp.(pad_hdata + k) <-
    50	      (if bit out.uio_oe k = 1 then out.pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k)
    51	       else match d with Some d -> rep4 (bit d k) | None -> 0)
    52	  done;
    53	  smp.(pad_hstb) <- rep4 s;
    54	  smp.(pad_hrd) <- rep4 r;
    55	  smp
    56	
    57	(* One clock with the samples [smp] taken in it: the core sees the samples of two clocks ago. *)
    58	let advance b (cores : core list) ~smp ~reset =
    59	  let seen = List.hd b.pipe in
    60	  b.pipe <- List.tl b.pipe @ [ smp ];
    61	  let outs = List.map (fun c -> c.step ~smp:seen ~reset) cores in
    62	  if b.keep_trace then b.trace <- (Array.copy b.out.pad_nib, b.out.uio_oe) :: b.trace;
    63	  b.out <- List.hd outs;
    64	  b.clock <- b.clock + 1;
    65	  outs

    10	   host would: it loads the store, the boot addresses, the pin map and the array through the
    11	   host link, sets run, and keeps the FIFOs fed and drained. *)

    22	   [on_clock] sees each clock's pad outputs after it. Returns whether they agreed throughout. *)
    23	let run_board ?(cfg = S.default_config) (b : Board.t) ~(world : world) ~clocks ~on_clock =
    24	  let spec = S.create ~cfg () and rtl = Chip_sim.create ~cfg () in

   147	  (* input: 997 Hz at -6 dB of the order's full scale (5567, ../onebit-dac/README.md) *)
   148	  let fs = 60e6 /. float Dacdemo.Dac.sample_clocks in
   149	  let left = Array.init samples (fun i -> int_of_float (Float.round (2783. *. sin (2. *. Float.pi *. 997. *. float i /. fs)))) in
   150	  let right = Array.make samples 0 in

   156	  let mem = Dacdemo.Dac.pump_programme () in
   157	  let used = Array.to_list (Array.sub mem 0 43) in
   158	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = 0; data = List.concat_map (fun w -> [ w land 0xFF; w lsr 8 ]) used });
   159	  let idle = Dacdemo.Dac.idle_pc in

   161	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_boot_pc 0; data = [ 0; idle; idle; idle; 0 ] });
   162	  (* the pin first, so that it shows F before the modulator starts: segment 3's flag, inverted
   163	     (F = 1 means y negative), on uo_out[0] *)
   164	  Host.wreg h (r_padsel 0) (src_tap_flag 3 lor 0x20);
   165	  let configured = ref max_int in

   167	  (* the array: the four PEs of order 2 (FB1, I1, FB2, I2: Dac.run_ops without its pass-through
   168	     PEs) as one run over segments 0-3; segment 0 is fed and repeats every 10 clocks, with the
   169	     lane loop; segments 1-3 join *)
   170	  let ops = Array.sub (Dacdemo.Dac.run_ops d) 4 4 in
   171	  for sg = 0 to 3 do
   172	    let bts = List.rev (Array.to_list (Upe.Spec.bytes_of_op ops.(sg))) in
   173	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_pecfg; addr = sg lsl 8; data = bts })
   174	  done;
   175	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = 2; data = [ 2 lor 16 lor 64 ] });
   176	  for sg = 1 to 3 do Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (sg lsl 2) lor 2; data = [ 16 ] }) done;
   177	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = 3; data = [ Dacdemo.Dac.period ] });
   178	  (* prefill four frames, then run *)
   179	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_hostin; addr = 0; data = List.init 16 (fun i -> bytes.(i)) });
   180	  Host.wreg h r_ctrl 3;
   181	  (* then keep the FIFO fed: read its count, top it up to 16, and collect underrun reports *)
   182	  let next = ref 16 and underruns = ref 0 and overflow = ref false in
   183	  let rec feed () =
   184	    if !next < Array.length bytes then begin
   185	      Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_hostin_count; n = 1; k = (fun l ->
   186	          let free = 16 - List.hd l in
   187	          let n = min (free land lnot 3) (Array.length bytes - !next) in
   188	          if n > 0 then begin
   189	            Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_hostin; addr = 0; data = List.init n (fun i -> bytes.(!next + i)) });
   190	            next := !next + n
   191	          end;
   192	          Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_hostout; addr = 0; n = 2; k = (fun l ->
   193	              match l with t :: _ when t land 0x80 <> 0 -> incr underruns | _ -> ()) });
   194	          Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_status; n = 1; k = (fun l ->
   195	              if List.hd l land 2 <> 0 then overflow := true) });
   196	          Host.submit h (Host.Idle 1000);
   197	          Host.submit h (Host.Call feed)) })
   198	    end
   199	  in
   200	  Host.submit h (Host.Call feed);
   201	  let total = 5000 + (samples * Dacdemo.Dac.sample_clocks) in
   202	  let pin = Array.make total 0 in
   203	  let agree, spec =
   204	    run_board b ~world:(fun ~clock:_ _ -> Array.make n_pads 0) ~clocks:total
   205	      ~on_clock:(fun c (o : S.outputs) -> pin.(c) <- (o.pad_nib.(0) lsr 3) land 1)
   206	  in
   207	  if Sys.getenv_opt "DAC_DEBUG" <> None then begin
   208	    let st = Upe.Model.state spec.pe in
   209	    Array.iteri (fun i (p : Upe.Spec.pe_state) -> pr "  PE%d %s\n" i (Upe.Spec.pe_state_to_string p)) st.pes;
   210	    Array.iteri (fun j (g : Upe.Spec.seg_state) -> pr "  seg%d flo %02x fhi %02x ctrl %02x rep %d fw %04x\n" j g.flo g.fhi g.ctrl g.rep g.fw) st.segs;
   211	    pr "  regs ctrl %x padsel0 %x; seq pcs %s\n" spec.regs.(r_ctrl) spec.regs.(r_padsel 0)
   212	      (String.concat " " (Array.to_list (Array.map string_of_int spec.seq.pcs)))
   213	  end;
   214	  pr "  RTL and specification agree on every pad, every clock: %b\n" agree;
   215	  pr "  host: %d of %d bytes sent, underrun reports %d, HOSTIN overflow %b\n" !next (Array.length bytes) !underruns !overflow;
   216	  (* the pin's bitstream: one bit per 10 clocks. The pin changes only on one clock phase mod 10;
   217	     sample half a step later. *)
   218	  let changes = Array.make 10 0 in
   219	  for c = 1 to total - 1 do if pin.(c) <> pin.(c - 1) then changes.(c mod 10) <- changes.(c mod 10) + 1 done;
   220	  let phase = ref 0 in
   221	  Array.iteri (fun k n -> if n > changes.(!phase) then phase := k) changes;
   222	  let off = (!phase + 5) mod 10 in
   223	  pr "  pin edges by clock phase mod 10: %s (sampled at phase %d)\n"
   224	    (String.concat " " (Array.to_list (Array.map string_of_int changes))) off;
   225	  let bits = Array.init ((total - off) / 10) (fun j -> 1 - pin.(off + (10 * j))) in   (* back to F *)
   226	  (* The reference: the fast model on the same words, each held 136 steps. Before the first
   227	     commit the feed repeats its initial word 0, so the model first takes [lead] steps on 0; that
   228	     number is the only parameter fitted. Before the modulator starts (the host is still
   229	     configuring it) the pin shows F = 0; the start is placed exactly, from where the pin first
   230	     shows a 1 against where the model on zeros first outputs a 1. *)
   231	  let steps = Dacdemo.Dac.sample_clocks / Dacdemo.Dac.period in
   232	  let first_one a = let rec go j = if j >= Array.length a then -1 else if a.(j) = 1 then j else go (j + 1) in go 0 in
   233	  let r1 = let f = Dacdemo.Dac.fast_create d in first_one (Array.init 1000 (fun _ -> if Dacdemo.Dac.fast_step f 0 then 1 else 0)) in
   234	  let from = !configured / 10 + 1 in
   235	  let start = (from + first_one (Array.sub bits from (Array.length bits - from))) - r1 in
   236	  let reference lead =
   237	    let f = Dacdemo.Dac.fast_create d in
   238	    let n = Array.length bits in
   239	    Array.init n (fun j ->
   240	        let j = j - start in
   241	        if j < 0 then 0
   242	        else
   243	          let w = if j < lead then 0 else let s = (j - lead) / steps in if s < samples then left.(s) land 0xFFFF else left.(samples - 1) land 0xFFFF in
   244	          if Dacdemo.Dac.fast_step f w then 1 else 0)
   245	  in
   246	  let judged = start + ((samples - 1) * steps) in
   247	  let best = ref (-1, max_int) in
   248	  for lead = 0 to 2000 do
   249	    let r = reference lead in
   250	    let wrong = ref 0 in
   251	    for j = from to min (Array.length bits) (lead + judged) - 1 do if r.(j) <> bits.(j) then incr wrong done;
   252	    if !wrong < snd !best then best := (lead, !wrong)
   253	  done;
   254	  let lead, wrong = !best in
   255	  let n = min (Array.length bits) (lead + judged) in
   256	  if Sys.getenv_opt "DAC_DEBUG" <> None then begin
   257	    let r = reference lead in
   258	    let w = ref [] in
   259	    for j = n - 1 downto from do if r.(j) <> bits.(j) then w := j :: !w done;
   260	    pr "  wrong at steps: %s\n" (String.concat " " (List.map string_of_int (List.filteri (fun i _ -> i < 40) !w)))
   261	  end;
   262	  pr "  bitstream: %d steps compared with ../onebit-dac's fast model (pin configured by step %d, modulator started at step %d; lead-in of %d steps on word 0 fitted): %d wrong\n"
   263	    (n - from) from start lead wrong;
   264	  (* a second fit as a control: the same comparison against the wrong order must fail *)
   265	  let ctrl =
   266	    let f = Dacdemo.Dac.fast_create (Dacdemo.Dac.design_of "o3") in
   267	    let wrong = ref 0 in
   268	    for j = start to n - 1 do
   269	      let k = j - start in
   270	      let w = if k < lead then 0 else left.(min (samples - 1) ((k - lead) / steps)) land 0xFFFF in
   271	      if (if Dacdemo.Dac.fast_step f w then 1 else 0) <> bits.(j) then incr wrong
   272	    done;
   273	    !wrong
   274	  in
   275	  pr "  control: the same bits against the order-3 model: %d of %d wrong\n" ctrl n;
   276	  let ok = agree && start > from && wrong = 0 && n > (samples - 2) * steps && !underruns = 0 && not !overflow && ctrl > n / 10 in
   277	  pr "one-bit DAC on the combined chip: %s\n%!" (if ok then "PASS" else "FAIL");
   278	  ok
   279	
   280	let () =
   281	  match Array.to_list Sys.argv with
   282	  | [ _; "ds" ] -> exit (if ds () then 0 else 1)
   283	  | [ _; "ds"; dir ] -> exit (if ds ~trace_dir:dir () then 0 else 1)
   284	  | [ _; "dac" ] -> exit (if dac ~samples:200 then 0 else 1)
   285	  | [ _; "dac"; n ] -> exit (if dac ~samples:(int_of_string n) then 0 else 1)
   286	  | _ -> prerr_endline "usage: demos.exe ds [TRACE_DIR] | dac [SAMPLES]"

    41	    Set_d (b land 15); Wait write_setup; Toggle_s; Wait write_hold ]

    43	let cmd t ~op ~tgt ~addr ~count =
    44	  List.iter (fun b -> push_steps t (byte_steps b))
    45	    [ (op lsl 4) lor tgt; (addr lsr 8) land 0xFF; addr land 0xFF; count - 1 ]

    47	let submit t = function
    48	  | Write { tgt; addr; data } ->
    49	    (* at most 256 bytes per command *)
    50	    let rec go addr = function
    51	      | [] -> ()
    52	      | l ->
    53	        let chunk = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i < 256) l and rest = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i >= 256) l in
    54	        cmd t ~op:op_write ~tgt ~addr ~count:(List.length chunk);
    55	        List.iter (fun b -> push_steps t (byte_steps b)) chunk;
    56	        go (addr + List.length chunk) rest
    57	    in
    58	    go addr data
    59	  | Read { tgt; addr; n; k } ->
    60	    assert (n >= 1 && n <= 256);
    61	    cmd t ~op:op_read ~tgt ~addr ~count:n;
    62	    let acc = ref [] and hi = ref 0 in
    63	    push_steps t [ Release_d; Wait turnaround; Set_r 1; Wait read_gap ];

    65	      push_steps t
    66	        [ Sample (fun v -> hi := v); Toggle_s; Wait read_gap;
    67	          Sample (fun v -> acc := ((!hi lsl 4) lor v) :: !acc); Toggle_s; Wait read_gap ]
    68	    done;
    69	    push_steps t [ Set_r 0; Wait turnaround; Do (fun () -> k (List.rev !acc)) ]
    70	  | Idle n -> push_steps t [ Wait n ]
    71	  | Call f -> push_steps t [ Do f ]

    73	(* One clock. [d_in] is the level of the four data lines during this clock (whoever drives
    74	   them). Returns what the host drives during this clock: (S, R, D or None). *)
    75	let clock t ~d_in =
    76	  if t.wait > 0 then t.wait <- t.wait - 1
    77	  else begin
    78	    let continue = ref true in
    79	    while !continue && not (Queue.is_empty t.q) do
    80	      match Queue.pop t.q with
    81	      | Set_d v -> t.d <- Some v
    82	      | Release_d -> t.d <- None
    83	      | Toggle_s -> t.s <- 1 - t.s
    84	      | Set_r v -> t.r <- v
    85	      | Wait n -> t.wait <- n - 1; continue := false
    86	      | Sample f -> f d_in
    87	      | Do f -> f ()
    88	    done
    89	  end;
    90	  if not (idle t) then t.busy_clocks <- t.busy_clocks + 1;
    91	  (t.s, t.r, t.d)

     1	(* The Tiny Tapeout top level: the chip core between the four-phase pin stage
     2	   (../multiphase/stage.ml, unchanged) and Tiny Tapeout's pins.
     3	
     4	   Phases: Tiny Tapeout gives one clock and no delay line is placed yet (README, "Phases"), so the
     5	   stage runs on both clock edges: phases 0 and 1 on the rising edge, 2 and 3 on the falling edge.
     6	   The core's nibbles are folded onto the half grid (n0 n0 n2 n2), so lanes 1 and 3 never toggle,
     7	   and the input samples of quarters 1 and 3 are taken at the same instants as 0 and 2.
     8

    10

/bin/bash -lc "nice ionice -c 3 nl -ba README.md | sed -n '75,180p'" in /home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top
 succeeded in 0ms:
    75	sampler reads one selected pad's four quarter samples, with an optional "active" (squelch) pad.
    76	
    77	**Phases.** The core is one clock domain. Its outputs are, per output pad, the nibble of levels for
    78	the four quarters of the clock, and its inputs, per input pad, the four quarter samples; the
    79	four-phase stage (`../multiphase/stage.ml`, unchanged) sits between them and the pins. Its
    80	invariants, which `../multiphase` verified, set the simulation boundary: the pad shows nibble k
    81	during clock k + 1, and the core sees in clock j + 2 the samples taken in clock j. Tiny Tapeout
    82	gives one clock, and the note's quarter phases need the calibrated delay line (deferred). The
    83	hardened chip therefore runs the stage on **both clock edges**: phases 0 and 1 on the rising
    84	edge, 2 and 3 on the falling edge, and the core's nibble folded onto the half grid
    85	(`n0 n0 n2 n2`), so lanes 1 and 3 never toggle. An edge requested at quarter 1 or 3 then appears a
    86	quarter late. This is the note's "both clock edges first (free)" step, marked here as a
    87	deviation of the hardened chip from the simulated one at the pins only.
    88	
    89	### Host link protocol
    90	
    91	Four data lines `D` (`uio[3:0]`), a strobe `S` from the host (`uio[4]`), and a read request `R`
    92	(`uio[5]`). The chip sees all three through the input stage's synchronisers (the latest quarter
    93	sample). The host clocks the chip (the RP2040/RP2350 makes the 60 MHz), so all timing is in chip
    94	clocks.
    95	
    96	- **Every change of S moves one nibble**, in either direction. Bytes go high nibble first.
    97	- **Write (R low).** The host sets D, waits at least 2 clocks, toggles S, and holds D for at least
    98	  2 more clocks. The chip takes D in the clock in which it sees S change.
    99	- **Read (R high).** The chip drives D with the current nibble. The host samples D, then toggles S
   100	  to ask for the next nibble, with at least 8 clocks between toggles (input latency, the read and
   101	  the output stage). Before raising R the host releases D; after lowering R it waits 8 clocks
   102	  before driving D.
   103	- **Commands** are bytes in the write direction: `{op, target}`, then address high, address low
   104	  and a count byte n, which means n + 1 bytes. op 1 writes the n + 1 data bytes that follow; op 2
   105	  reads: the host raises R and reads 2 (n + 1) nibbles. Each data byte goes to (target, address)
   106	  and the address increments. Lowering R returns the command parser to idle, which is also the way to
   107	  resynchronise.
   108	
   109	| target | write | read |
   110	|---|---|---|
   111	| 0 REG | configuration and control registers (map in `regs.ml`) | registers and status |
   112	| 1 PROG | programme store, byte address (word a/2; low byte at even a; the word is written with its high byte). Only while the sequencer is stopped; otherwise ignored and a sticky error bit is set | the same bytes, while stopped |
   113	| 2 BANK | data bank, while stopped | the same |
   114	| 3 HOSTIN | push the byte into the 16-byte FIFO that the threads' IN reads (dropped, with a sticky bit, when full) | |
   115	| 4 HOSTOUT | | pops the 8-entry FIFO filled by OUT: two bytes per entry, `{valid, tag}` then the byte |
   116	| 5 PECFG | one byte into the configuration chain of segment (address bits 9:8) | |
   117	| 6 PEINIT | one byte into the init chain of segment (address bits 9:8) | |
   118	| 7 PESEG | segment (address / 4 mod 4) register (address mod 4): feed low, feed high, control, repeat | |
   119	| 8 STREAM | three bytes per word: low, high, vector count; the word is pushed with its count byte | |
   120	| 9 SAMPLE | | pops the sampler FIFO: three bytes per entry, `{valid, count}`, low, high |
   121	| 10 MATCH | bit 0 of the byte is shifted into the matcher's configuration chain | |
   122	
   123	Control: register 0 bit 0 is **run**. While it is clear the sequencer is held in clear, and its
   124	memory and FIFO strobes are gated off (the core's strobes are combinational of the fetched word,
   125	and the clear does not mask them). Register 0 bit 1 holds the bit-path assists, streamer,
   126	sampler and NCO in clear. Each thread's boot page and pc are registers; another register
   127	restarts one thread at a page and pc while running (the core's `ctl` port).
   128	
   129	**How the programme store is loaded.** Hold run clear, write the boot pages and pcs, write the
   130	programme through PROG (and any tables through BANK), configure the array through PECFG, PEINIT
   131	and PESEG and the pins through REG, then set run. Programmes for the earlier ISA variants are
   132	translated once by the host (`../sequencer-v2/compat.ml`) and relocated into the store, which is
   133	the compiler's job anyway.
   134	
   135	### The thread ↔ array glue (MBX ports 4-7 ↔ segments 0-3)
   136	
   137	- **SEND on port p** (always ready): the byte goes into segment p's feed register, alternately
   138	  as the low byte and as the high byte; the high byte commits the word (as the note says and as
   139	  the one-bit DAC's pump assumes). The SEND reaches the array one clock after it executes,
   140	  because the core registers its port output. A host write to the same path waits a clock if a
   141	  thread's byte is going in.
   142	- **RECV on port p**: a holding register per port takes segment p's tap word whenever it is
   143	  empty and the tap is valid; RECV returns its low byte, then its high byte, then it is empty.
   144	- Segment taps' flags and valid bits are flag sources; tap flags and tap bit 0 are pad sources.
   145	
   146	### Sizes and how the PE count grows
   147	
   148	Simulation: the 1024-word store, the 1024-byte bank and any layout. The hardened chip starts at
   149	**4 PEs** (segments 1|1|1|1, so that all four MBX ports have a segment) and grows towards 16
   150	(2|2|2|2 for 8, then the note's 2|2|4|8) while the measured area allows. The programme store
   151	and bank sizes are parameters of the hardened top; which memory implementation is used is
   152	recorded with each harden.
   153	
   154	### Verification plan
   155	
   156	1. **Executable specification** (`chip_spec.ml`): the blocks' own models stepped together: ISA
   157	   v2's interpreter (`isa2.ml`), the PE array model, the streamer and sampler models, the edge
   158	   sampler, CRC and matcher models; new code only for the glue (host link, FIFOs, registers,
   159	   pin map, port holds) and for the NCO, which has RTL but no model.
   160	2. **Lockstep** (`lockstep.ml`): RTL against the specification every clock, on random programmes,
   161	   random pin inputs and random host-link traffic (including programme loads and array
   162	   configuration), comparing every pad, the host link's read data and the architectural state of
   163	   the sequencer and the array.
   164	3. **Planted integration bugs**, each of which the lockstep must catch: swapped pin lanes, a
   165	   wrong mailbox port wiring, a miswired segment, a flag select off by one, a host-link nibble
   166	   order, ungated strobes during clear, and more.
   167	4. **Demos, unchanged firmware, through the host link** (`demos.ml`): UART, SPI and I2C from
   168	   `../deadline-sequencer` with its decoders and I2C slave; the same pins to
   169	   `../../tools/sigrok-judge`; then the one-bit DAC from `../onebit-dac` end to end, judged
   170	   bit for bit against its own fast model.
   171	5. **Area and G13**: synthesis, then `../../tt/scripts/harden.sh` at 6x4, reporting the
   172	   placement factor against the note's 1.5 and 2.0 and the PE count that fits; then
   173	   `../postlayout-roundtrip`'s lockstep and `compare_def` on the hardened layout if practical.
   174	
   175	Generated Verilog is never committed (as `../../tt/scripts/regen.sh` does).

     1	(* Lockstep of the chip core's RTL against its executable specification, every clock, on random
     2	   programmes, random pad inputs and random host-link traffic; then the planted integration bugs,
     3	   each of which must be caught.
     4	
     5	     lockstep.exe run TRIALS CLOCKS [SEED]      the design
     6	     lockstep.exe controls TRIALS CLOCKS        every planted bug
     7	     lockstep.exe layouts TRIALS CLOCKS         the design at 4, 8 and 16 PEs
     8	
     9	   Compared every clock: every output pad's nibble and the uio output enables (what the pins
    10	   show); and the architectural state of the sequencer and of every PE, the host FIFOs' counts,
    11	   the CRC register, the NCO's output register, the matcher's sum, the edge sampler's outputs
    12	   and the host link's read buffer. *)
    13	
    14	open Regs
    15	module S = Chip_spec
    16	
    17	let pr = Printf.printf
    18	
    19	(* ---- random programmes ---- *)
    20	
    21	(* Generators, chosen per trial (seed mod the number of modes), each biased towards the paths
    22	   that uniform traffic reaches rarely:
    23	   - mixed: everything;
    24	   - ports: threads SEND and RECV on the ports while the host writes the same segments' feeds;
    25	   - host: threads OUT and IN while the host polls HOSTOUT and fills HOSTIN;
    26	   - memory: the threads' first words are memory and FIFO instructions (fetched again on every
    27	     clock in clear), and the host reads and writes the bank and the store while stopped;
    28	   - race: rare OUTs while the host polls HOSTOUT without pause, so that an entry arrives
    29	     between the two bytes of a read. *)
    30	let modes = [| "mixed"; "ports"; "host"; "memory"; "race" |]
    31	
    32	let gen_programme ?(mode = "mixed") r ~words ~base =
    33	  let i n = Random.State.int r n and chance p = Random.State.float r 1.0 < p in
    34	  let a () = base + i words in
    35	  let pick l = List.nth l (i (List.length l)) in
    36	  Array.init words (fun k ->
    37	      let x = i 100 in
    38	      let x =
    39	        match mode with
    40	        | "ports" -> if chance 0.5 then 53 + i 16 else x
    41	        | "host" -> if chance 0.4 then (if i 3 = 0 then 50 else 45 + i 5) else x
    42	        | "memory" -> if k = 0 then List.nth [ 87; 87; 83; 50; 64 ] (i 5) else x
    43	        | "race" -> if x >= 45 && x < 50 && i 4 > 0 then 37 else if x < 28 && x >= 23 then 24 else x
    44	        | _ -> x
    45	      in
    46	      if x < 9 then Isa2.setp ~q:(i 4) ~mask:(i 256) ~value:(i 2) ~oe:(i 2) ()
    47	      else if x < 16 then Isa2.sho ~od:(i 2) ~pair:(i 2) ~psel:(i 2) ~cap:(i 2) ~q:(i 4) ~pin:(i 8) ~msb:(i 2) ()
    48	      else if x < 19 then Isa2.shi ~quad:(i 2) ~pin:(i 8) ~msb:(i 2) ()
    49	      else if x < 23 then Isa2.ldc (i 20)
    50	      else if x < 28 then Isa2.ldd (if chance 0.7 then 0 else i 30)
    51	      else if x < 33 then Isa2.lda (if chance 0.5 then i 8 else i 256)
    52	      else if x < 36 then Isa2.waitp ~pin:(i 8) ~value:(i 2) ~fail:(a ())
    53	      else if x < 38 then Isa2.waitd
    54	      else if x < 42 then Isa2.jmp (a ())
    55	      else if x < 45 then Isa2.jnz (a ())
    56	      else if x < 50 then (if chance 0.5 then Isa2.out ~tag:(i 8) () else Isa2.outi ~tag:(i 8) (i 256))
    57	      else if x < 53 then Isa2.in_
    58	      else if x < 62 then Isa2.send ~ch:(pick [ 4; 5; 6; 7; 4; 5; 0; 1; 2; 3 ]) ~fail:(a ())
    59	      else if x < 69 then Isa2.recv ~ch:(pick [ 4; 5; 6; 7; 4; 5; 0; 1; 2; 3 ]) ~fail:(a ())
    60	      else if x < 76 then Isa2.waitc ~cond:(pick [ 12; 13; 14; 15; 12; 13; 9; 10; 11; i 16 ]) ~fail:(a ())
    61	      else if x < 79 then Isa2.skne (i 4)
    62	      else if x < 81 then Isa2.skeq (i 4)
    63	      else if x < 83 then Isa2.cnta
    64	      else if x < 87 then Isa2.ldb
    65	      else if x < 91 then Isa2.stb
    66	      else if x < 93 then Isa2.bank (i 4)
    67	      else if x < 95 then Isa2.cfg (if chance 0.5 then Isa2.cfg_round_latch else i 128)
    68	      else if x < 96 then Isa2.fine (i 256)
    69	      else Isa2.nop)
    70	
    71	(* ---- random PE configurations (from the array's own generator) ---- *)
    72	
    73	let rand_op r : Upe.Spec.op =
    74	  let i n = Random.State.int r n and b () = Random.State.bool r in
    75	  { xsel = i 4; sinsel = i 4; ysel = i 4; ymod = i 4; gsel = i 8; bitsel = i 16; pairlo = b (); alu = i 8;
    76	    cin_lane = b (); swb = i 4; pwb = i 4; fwb = i 4; lout = i 4; lane_bc = b (); del = Random.State.int r 4 = 0;
    77	    stream = b (); tap_p = b (); follow = Random.State.int r 4 = 0; ashr = (if b () then 0 else i 16); k = i 0x10000 }
    78	
    79	(* ---- one trial ---- *)

    87	let state_diffs (s : S.t) (r : Chip_sim.t) =
    88	  let d = ref [] in
    89	  let cmp name a b = if a <> b then d := Printf.sprintf "%s: spec %x rtl %x" name a b :: !d in
    90	  let per name w = Array.init 4 (fun t -> (Chip_sim.get r ("seq_dbg_" ^ name) lsr (w * t)) land ((1 lsl w) - 1)) in
    91	  let pend = Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_pend" in
    92	  let st = s.seq in
    93	  let cmp4 name w (a : int array) =
    94	    let b = per name w in
    95	    Array.iteri (fun t x -> if not (name = "acc" && pend land 4 <> 0 && pend land 3 = t) then cmp (Printf.sprintf "seq %s%d" name t) x b.(t)) a
    96	  in
    97	  cmp4 "pc" 8 st.pcs; cmp4 "page" 2 st.pages; cmp4 "acc" 8 st.accs; cmp4 "cnt" 12 st.cnts; cmp4 "dl" 12 st.dls;
    98	  cmp4 "bp" 10 st.bps; cmp4 "fine" 8 st.fines; cmp4 "armed" 1 st.armed; cmp4 "cfg" 8 st.cfgs;
    99	  cmp4 "lsend" 3 st.lsend; cmp4 "inbox" 8 st.inbox; cmp4 "full" 1 st.full;
   100	  cmp "seq thread" st.thread (Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_thread");
   101	  cmp "seq latch" st.latch (Chip_sim.get r "seq_dbg_latch");
   102	  let pes = (Upe.Model.state s.pe).pes in
   103	  Array.iteri
   104	    (fun i (p : Upe.Spec.pe_state) ->
   105	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d S" i) p.s (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_s%d" i));
   106	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d P" i) p.p (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_p%d" i));
   107	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d Pv" i) (Bool.to_int p.pv) (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_pv%d" i));
   108	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d F" i) (Bool.to_int p.f) (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_f%d" i));
   109	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d L" i) (Bool.to_int p.l) (Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "pe_l%d" i));
   110	      let c = Chip_sim.get_bits r (Printf.sprintf "pe_cfg%d" i) in
   111	      Array.iteri (fun j v -> cmp (Printf.sprintf "pe%d cfg%d" i j) v (Hardcaml.Bits.to_int (Hardcaml.Bits.select c (8 * j + 7) (8 * j)))) p.cfg)
   112	    pes;
   113	  Array.iteri
   114	    (fun j (g : Upe.Spec.seg_state) ->
   115	      let get n = Chip_sim.get r (Printf.sprintf "seg_%s%d" n j) in
   116	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d feed low" j) g.flo (get "flo"); cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d feed high" j) g.fhi (get "fhi");
   117	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d feed valid" j) (Bool.to_int g.fv) (get "fv"); cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d control" j) g.ctrl (get "ctrl");
   118	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d repeat" j) g.rep (get "rep"); cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d repeat count" j) g.cnt (get "cnt");
   119	      cmp (Printf.sprintf "seg%d committed word" j) g.fw (get "fw"))
   120	    (Upe.Model.state s.pe).segs;
   121	  cmp "send byte order" (Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.mapi (fun p b -> Bool.to_int b lsl p) s.send_hi)) (Chip_sim.get r "send_hi");
   122	  let holds = Chip_sim.get_bits r "holds" in
   123	  for p = 0 to 3 do
   124	    let h = Hardcaml.Bits.to_int (Hardcaml.Bits.select holds (18 * p + 17) (18 * p)) in
   125	    cmp (Printf.sprintf "port %d hold state" p) s.hold_st.(p) (h land 3);
   126	    if s.hold_st.(p) <> 0 then cmp (Printf.sprintf "port %d hold word" p) s.hold_w.(p) (h lsr 2)
   127	  done;
   128	  cmp "hostin count" (Queue.length s.hostin) (Chip_sim.get r "hin_count");
   129	  cmp "hostout count" (Queue.length s.hostout) (Chip_sim.get r "hout_count");
   130	  cmp "crc" s.crc (Chip_sim.get r "crc");
   131	  cmp "nco" s.nco_out (Chip_sim.get r "nco");
   132	  cmp "matcher sum" (fst (S.matcher_now s)) (Chip_sim.get r "match_y");
   133	  cmp "rbuf" s.rbuf (Chip_sim.get r "rbuf");
   134	  let e = s.es_out in
   135	  cmp "edge sampler" (((if e.valid then e.bit else 0) lsl 3) lor (Bool.to_int e.valid lsl 2) lor (Bool.to_int e.burst_end lsl 1) lor Bool.to_int s.es.in_burst)
   136	    (Chip_sim.get r "es");
   137	  cmp "edge sampler last bit" s.es_last (Chip_sim.get r "es_last");
   138	  List.rev !d
   139	
   140	let out_diffs (a : S.outputs) (b : S.outputs) =
   141	  let d = ref [] in
   142	  Array.iteri (fun i x -> if x <> b.pad_nib.(i) then d := Printf.sprintf "pad %d: spec %x rtl %x" i x b.pad_nib.(i) :: !d) a.pad_nib;
   143	  if a.uio_oe <> b.uio_oe then d := Printf.sprintf "uio_oe: spec %02x rtl %02x" a.uio_oe b.uio_oe :: !d;
   144	  List.rev !d
