   146	(* the host's random traffic: a setup, then transactions while the programme runs *)
   147	let setup_traffic ?(mode = "mixed") r (h : Host.t) ~(cfg : S.config) =
   148	  let i n = Random.State.int r n and chance p = Random.State.float r 1.0 < p in
   149	  let w = Host.wreg h in
   150	  let block a l = Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_reg; addr = a; data = l }) in
   151	  w r_ctrl 2;                                   (* stopped, assists held *)
   152	  (* the programme: each thread a region of a page *)
   153	  let pages = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> i 4) and bases = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> i 224) in
   154	  for t = 0 to 3 do
   155	    let words = gen_programme ~mode r ~words:(8 + i 24) ~base:bases.(t) in
   156	    let addr = ((pages.(t) lsl 8) lor bases.(t)) land (cfg.prog_words - 1) in
   157	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = 2 * addr;
   158	                                data = List.concat_map (fun w -> [ w land 0xFF; w lsr 8 ]) (Array.to_list words) })
   159	  done;
   160	  block (r_boot_pc 0) (Array.to_list bases @ [ Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.mapi (fun t p -> p lsl (2 * t)) pages) ]);
   161	  (* pads, pins, sampler pins, flags *)
   162	  block (r_padsel 0)
   163	    (List.init 16 (fun _ -> i 128) @ List.init 8 (fun _ -> i 16) @ List.init 4 (fun _ -> i 16) @ [ i 2 ]);
   164	  block (r_flagsel 0) (List.init 16 (fun _ -> if chance 0.8 then i 17 else i 32));
   165	  (* the assists, 0x40..0x6F *)
   166	  let width () = List.nth [ 1; 2; 4 ] (i 3) in
   167	  let width_crc = List.nth [ 5; 8; 15; 16; 32 ] (i 5) in
   168	  let mask = if width_crc = 32 then 0xFFFF_FFFF else (1 lsl width_crc) - 1 in
   169	  let le32 v = List.init 4 (fun k -> (v lsr (8 * k)) land 0xFF) in
   170	  let rb () = Random.State.bits r in
   171	  block r_str_per
   172	    ([ 1 + i 6; width () lsl 4; i 256; i 16 ]                                    (* 0x40 streamer *)
   173	     @ [ 1 + i 6; (width () lsl 4) lor (i 2 lsl 7); i 6; (i 4 lsl 4) lor (i 2 lsl 6); i 12 ] (* 0x44 sampler *)
   174	     @ [ 0; 0; 0 ]                                                               (* 0x49-0x4B *)
   175	     @ [ i 16; i 256; 4 + i 12; 0; 8 + i 60; i 2; 4 + i 8; 4 + i 12 ]           (* 0x4C edge sampler *)
   176	     @ [ i 4; 0; 0; 0 ]                                                          (* 0x54 CRC control *)
   177	     @ le32 (rb () land mask) @ le32 (rb () land mask) @ le32 mask @ le32 (rb ())
   178	     @ le32 (if chance 0.5 then 0 else rb () land mask)
   179	     @ le32 (rb () lor (rb () lsl 30)));                                         (* 0x6C NCO *)
   180	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_match; addr = 0; data = List.init 37 (fun _ -> i 2) });
   181	  (* the array: configuration and init chains, segment registers *)
   182	  let l = cfg.layout in
   183	  for sg = 0 to 3 do
   184	    let n = l.end_.(sg) - l.start.(sg) + 1 in
   185	    let bytes = List.concat (List.init n (fun _ -> List.rev (Array.to_list (Upe.Spec.bytes_of_op (rand_op r))))) in
   186	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_pecfg; addr = sg lsl 8; data = bytes });
   187	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peinit; addr = sg lsl 8; data = List.init (2 * n) (fun _ -> i 256) });
   188	    let src = if mode = "ports" then List.nth [ 2; 2; 2; 0 ] (i 4) else List.nth [ 0; 0; 1; 2; 2; 3; 5 ] (i 7) in
   189	    let ctrl = src lor (i 2 lsl 3) lor (if chance 0.85 then 16 else 0) lor (i 2 lsl 5) lor (i 2 lsl 6) in
   190	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (sg lsl 2) lor 2; data = [ ctrl ] });
   191	    if chance 0.4 then Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (sg lsl 2) lor 3; data = [ List.nth [ 1; 2; 3; 5; 9 ] (i 5) ] })
   192	  done;
   193	  Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_bank; addr = (if mode = "memory" then 0 else i 1024); data = List.init 16 (fun _ -> i 256) });
   194	  w r_ctrl 0;                                    (* release the assists *)
   195	  w r_ctrl 1                                     (* run *)
   196	
   197	let running_traffic ?(mode = "mixed") r (h : Host.t) =
   198	  let i n = Random.State.int r n in
   199	  let w = Host.wreg h in
   200	  let ignore_k _ = () in
   201	  let x = i 22 in
   202	  let x =
   203	    match mode with
   204	    | "ports" -> if i 2 = 0 then List.nth [ 9; 10; 13 ] (i 3) else x
   205	    | "host" -> if i 2 = 0 then List.nth [ 0; 3; 4; 3 ] (i 4) else x
   206	    | "memory" -> if i 2 = 0 then 18 else x
   207	    | "race" -> if i 4 > 0 then 3 else x
   208	    | _ -> x
   209	  in
   210	  match x with
   211	  | 0 | 1 | 2 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_hostin; addr = 0; data = List.init (1 + i 4) (fun _ -> i 256) })
   212	  | 3 | 4 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_hostout; addr = 0; n = 2 * (1 + i 3); k = ignore_k })
   213	  | 5 | 6 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = i reg_space; n = 1 + i 4; k = ignore_k })
   214	  | 7 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_stream; addr = 0; data = [ i 256; i 256; i 16 ] })
   215	  | 8 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_sample; addr = 0; n = 4; k = ignore_k })
   216	  | 9 | 10 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_peseg; addr = (i 4 lsl 2) lor (List.nth [ 0; 1; 1; 2; 3 ] (i 5)); data = [ i 256 ] })
   217	  | 11 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = (if i 2 = 0 then t_pecfg else t_peinit); addr = i 4 lsl 8; data = [ i 256 ] })
   218	  | 12 -> w r_restart (i 16); w r_restart_pc (i 256)
   219	  | 13 -> w r_port_reset (i 16)
   220	  | 14 -> w r_crc_start 0
   221	  | 15 -> Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_status; n = 1; k = ignore_k }); w r_status 0
   222	  | 16 -> Host.wreg32 h r_nco_inc (Random.State.bits r)
   223	  | 17 -> w (r_flagsel (i 16)) (i 32); w (r_padsel (List.nth general_out_pads (i 10))) (i 128)
   224	  | 18 ->
   225	    (* stop, touch the memories, start again *)
   226	    w r_ctrl 0;
   227	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = 2 * i 1024; data = [ i 256; i 256 ] });
   228	    Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_prog; addr = i 2048; n = 2; k = ignore_k });
   229	    let ba () = if i 2 = 0 || mode = "memory" then i 4 else i 1024 in
   230	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_bank; addr = ba (); data = [ i 256 ] });
   231	    Host.submit h (Host.Idle (i 30));
   232	    Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_bank; addr = ba (); n = 2; k = ignore_k });
   233	    w r_ctrl 1
   234	  | 19 -> Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_prog; addr = i 2048; data = [ i 256 ] })   (* refused while running *)
   235	  | 20 ->
   236	    (* hold the assists, change their configuration, release *)
   237	    w r_ctrl 3; w r_es_mode (i 16); w r_smp_per (1 + i 5);
   238	    Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_match; addr = 0; data = [ i 2; i 2; i 2 ] }); w r_ctrl 1
   239	  | _ -> Host.submit h (Host.Idle (i 40))
   240

    48	(* A synchronous single-port memory: [addr], [we] and [wdata] in one clock; the word at [addr]
    49	   is on the output during the next clock. *)
    50	type mem = clock:Signal.t -> size:int -> width:int -> addr:Signal.t -> we:Signal.t -> wdata:Signal.t -> Signal.t
    51	
    52	let behavioural_mem : mem =
    53	 fun ~clock ~size ~width:_ ~addr ~we ~wdata ->
    54	  let abits = address_bits_for size in
    55	  let a = select addr (abits - 1) 0 in
    56	  let q = memory size ~write_port:{ write_clock = clock; write_address = a; write_enable = we; write_data = wdata }
    57	      ~read_address:(reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) a) in
    58	  q

    62	let behavioural = { prog_mem = behavioural_mem; bank_mem = behavioural_mem }

    66	(* a FIFO of [depth] entries; a push into a full FIFO is taken when the head leaves in the same
    67	   clock. Returns head, count, accept. *)
    68	let fifo spec ~depth ~push ~pop ~data =
    69	  let pbits = address_bits_for depth in
    70	  let rd = wire pbits and wr = wire pbits and count = wire (pbits + 1) in
    71	  let full = count ==:. depth and nonempty = count <>:. 0 in
    72	  let popping = pop &: nonempty in
    73	  let accept = push &: (~:full |: popping) in
    74	  let slots = List.init depth (fun i -> reg spec ~enable:(accept &: (wr ==:. i)) data) in
    75	  rd <== reg spec (mux2 popping (rd +:. 1) rd);
    76	  wr <== reg spec (mux2 accept (wr +:. 1) wr);
    77	  count <== reg spec (count +: uresize accept (pbits + 1) -: uresize popping (pbits + 1));
    78	  (mux rd slots, count, accept, full, nonempty)

    80	let onehot_index v = mux2 (bit v 0) (of_int ~width:2 0) (mux2 (bit v 1) (of_int ~width:2 1) (mux2 (bit v 2) (of_int ~width:2 2) (of_int ~width:2 3)))
    81	let rep4 b = repeat b 4
    82	let nib v i = select v (4 * i + 3) (4 * i)

    84	let create ?(cfg = config) ?(mems = behavioural) ~clock ~reset ~smp () =
    85	  let spec = Reg_spec.create ~clock ~clear:reset () in
    86	  let lvl i = bit smp (4 * i + 3) in
    87	  (* ---- the pending host action (decoded last clock), and the register file ---- *)
    88	  let act_valid = wire 1 and act_tgt = wire 4 and act_addr = wire 16 and act_byte = wire 8 in
    89	  let act_is tg = act_valid &: (act_tgt ==:. tg) &: ~:reset in
    90	  let act_reg_at a = act_is t_reg &: (act_addr ==:. a) in
    91	  let regs =
    92	    Array.init reg_space (fun a ->
    93	        let w = rw_bits a in
    94	        if w = 0 then zero 8
    95	        else
    96	          let rs = Reg_spec.override spec ~clear_to:(of_int ~width:8 (reset_value a)) in
    97	          let d = uresize (select act_byte (w - 1) 0) 8 in
    98	          reg rs ~enable:(act_reg_at a) d)
    99	  in
   100	  let reg32 a = concat_msb [ regs.(a + 3); regs.(a + 2); regs.(a + 1); regs.(a) ] in
   101	  let run = bit regs.(r_ctrl) 0 and hold = bit regs.(r_ctrl) 1 in
   102	  let pulse a = act_reg_at a in
   103	  (* ---- the sequencer ---- *)
   104	  let pin_sel k = select regs.(r_pinin k) 3 0 in
   105	  let pad_nib_in i = nib smp i in
   106	  let sel_pad sel = mux sel (List.init n_pads pad_nib_in) in
   107	  let pin_k k =
   108	    let k = if is "pinin_lane_swap" then (match k with 2 -> 3 | 3 -> 2 | k -> k) else k in
   109	    sel_pad (pin_sel k)
   110	  in
   111	  let pin_in = concat_lsb (List.init 8 (fun k -> bit (pin_k k) (if is "pinin_quarter0" then 0 else 3))) in
   112	  let pin_in4 = concat_lsb (List.init 8 (fun k -> pin_k k)) in
   113	  let imem_data = wire 16 and bank_rdata = wire 8 in
   114	  let hostin_head = wire 8 and hostin_nonempty = wire 1 in
   115	  let port_in = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> wire 8) and port_in_valid = wire 4 in
   116	  let flags = wire 16 in
   117	  let boot_pcs = List.init 4 (fun t -> regs.(r_boot_pc t)) in
   118	  let boot_pcs = if is "boot_pc_swap" then (match boot_pcs with [ a; b; c; d ] -> [ a; c; b; d ] | l -> l) else boot_pcs in
   119	  let restart = pulse r_restart in
   120	  let seq =
   121	    Sequencer2.create ~clock ~clear:(reset |: ~:run) ~imem_data ~pin_in ~pin_in4 ~host_in:hostin_head
   122	      ~host_in_valid:hostin_nonempty ~port_in ~port_in_valid ~port_out_ready:(ones 4) ~flags
   123	      ~ctl_valid:restart ~ctl_thread:(select act_byte 1 0)
   124	      ~ctl_page:(if is "restart_page" then zero 2 else select act_byte 3 2) ~ctl_pc:regs.(r_restart_pc)
   125	      ~boot_page:regs.(r_boot_page) ~boot_pc:(concat_lsb boot_pcs) ~bank_rdata ()
   126	  in
   127	  let gate s = if is "hostin_ungated" then s else s &: run in
   128	  let host_in_ready = gate seq.host_in_ready in
   129	  let port_in_ready = seq.port_in_ready &: repeat run 4 in
   130	  (* the core owns the bank port while it runs; in clear its strobes are combinational of the
   131	     fetched word and must not reach the bank (the planted bug lets them) *)
   132	  let core_owns_bank = if is "bank_ungated" then run |: seq.bank_we else run in
   133	  let core_bank_we = seq.bank_we &: core_owns_bank &: ~:reset in
   134	  (* ---- the host link: input side ---- *)
   135	  let s = lvl pad_hstb and rr = lvl pad_hrd in
   136	  let d = concat_lsb (List.init 4 (fun k -> lvl (pad_hdata + k))) in
   137	  let prev_s = reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) s and prev_r = reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) rr in
   138	  let open Always in
   139	  let nphase = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and hi_nib = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 in
   140	  let pst = Variable.reg spec ~width:3 in
   141	  let st_idle = 0 and st_ahi = 1 and st_alo = 2 and st_count = 3 and st_wdata = 4 and st_rdata = 5 in
   142	  let is_read = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and tgt = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 in
   143	  let addr = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 and remaining = Variable.reg spec ~width:9 in
   144	  let rbuf = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 and rphase = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   145	  let fetch_valid = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and fetch_tgt = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 and fetch_addr = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 in
   146	  let nf_valid = Variable.wire ~default:gnd and nf_addr = Variable.wire ~default:(zero 16) in
   147	  let na_valid = Variable.wire ~default:gnd and na_byte = Variable.wire ~default:(zero 8) in
   148	  let rbuf_zero = Variable.wire ~default:gnd in
   149	  let byte_in = if is "nibble_order" then concat_msb [ d; hi_nib.value ] else concat_msb [ hi_nib.value; d ] in
   150	  let s_edge = s ^: prev_s and r_edge = rr ^: prev_r in
   151	  compile
   152	    [ if_ r_edge
   153	        [ nphase <--. 0; rphase <--. 0; when_ (rr ==:. 0) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ]
   154	        [ when_ s_edge
   155	            [ if_ (rr ==:. 0)
   156	                [ if_ (nphase.value ==:. 0)
   157	                    [ hi_nib <-- d; nphase <--. 1 ]
   158	                    [ nphase <--. 0
   159	                    ; switch pst.value
   160	                        [ of_int ~width:3 st_idle,
   161	                          [ when_ ((select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_write) |: (select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_read))
   162	                              [ is_read <-- (select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_read); tgt <-- select byte_in 3 0
   163	                              ; pst <--. st_ahi ] ]
   164	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_ahi, [ addr <-- concat_msb [ byte_in; zero 8 ]; pst <--. st_alo ]
   165	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_alo, [ addr <-- concat_msb [ select addr.value 15 8; byte_in ]; pst <--. st_count ]
   166	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_count,
   167	                          [ remaining <-- uresize byte_in 9 +:. 1
   168	                          ; if_ is_read.value [ pst <--. st_rdata; nf_valid <-- vdd; nf_addr <-- addr.value ]
   169	                              [ pst <--. st_wdata ] ]
   170	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_wdata,
   171	                          [ na_valid <-- vdd; na_byte <-- byte_in
   172	                          ; addr <-- addr.value +:. 1; remaining <-- remaining.value -:. 1
   173	                          ; when_ (remaining.value ==:. 1) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ]
   174	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_rdata, [ pst <--. st_idle ] ] ] ]
   175	                [ if_ (rphase.value ==:. 0) [ rphase <--. 1 ]
   176	                    [ rphase <--. 0
   177	                    ; if_ ((pst.value ==:. st_rdata) &: (remaining.value >:. 1))
   178	                        [ remaining <-- remaining.value -:. 1; addr <-- addr.value +:. 1
   179	                        ; nf_valid <-- vdd; nf_addr <-- addr.value +:. 1 ]
   180	                        [ remaining <--. 0; rbuf_zero <-- vdd
   181	                        ; when_ (pst.value ==:. st_rdata) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ] ] ] ] ] ];
   182	  (* ---- fetches ---- *)
   183	  let next_fetch_tgt = tgt.value in
   184	  let fv = fetch_valid.value and ft = fetch_tgt.value and fa = fetch_addr.value in
   185	  let fetch_is tg = fv &: (ft ==:. tg) in
   186	  (* ---- the array and the ports ---- *)
   187	  let es_bit = wire 1 and es_valid = wire 1 in
   188	  let push_v = seq.port_out_valid and push_d = seq.port_out_data in
   189	  let thread_push = (push_v <>:. 0) &: ~:reset in
   190	  let push_port = onehot_index push_v in

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	(* The systolic matcher of ../systolic-matcher/matcher.ml, copied with one change: a datapath
     2	   enable. Every datapath register (samples, partial sums, hit) advances only when [enable] is high,
     3	   so the closed form of ../systolic-matcher/model.ml holds in enabled steps instead of clocks.
     4	   This lets the array take one sample per received bit (here from the edge sampler, 10 Mbit/s at
     5	   60 MHz) rather than one per clock. Proposed as an upstream change to the matcher: one enable on
     6	   the register spec, no new cells. *)
     7	open Hardcaml
     8	open Signal
     9	
    10	let n = Model.n
    11	
    12	let create ~clock ~clear ~enable ~x ~cfg_in ~cfg_shift =
    13	  let spec = Reg_spec.create ~clock ~clear () in
    14	  let cfg_spec = Reg_spec.create ~clock () in
    15	  let chain = ref cfg_in in
    16	  let cfg_reg () = let r = reg cfg_spec ~enable:cfg_shift !chain in chain := r; r in
    17	  let cells = Array.init n (fun _ -> let m = cfg_reg () in let t = cfg_reg () in (m, t)) in
    18	  let thr = concat_lsb (List.init 5 (fun _ -> cfg_reg ())) in
    19	  let xin = ref x and yin = ref (zero 5) in
    20	  Array.iter (fun (m, t) ->
    21	    let xa = reg spec ~enable !xin in
    22	    let xb = reg spec ~enable xa in
    23	    let w = m &: (xa ==: t) in
    24	    let y = reg spec ~enable (!yin +: uresize w 5) in
    25	    xin := xb; yin := y) cells;
    26	  let y = !yin in
    27	  let hit = reg spec ~enable (y >=: thr) in
    28	  y, hit
    29	
    30	let circuit () =
    31	  let clock = input "clock" 1 and clear = input "clear" 1 and enable = input "enable" 1 in
    32	  let x = input "x" 1 and cfg_in = input "cfg_in" 1 and cfg_shift = input "cfg_shift" 1 in
    33	  let y, hit = create ~clock ~clear ~enable ~x ~cfg_in ~cfg_shift in

codex
I found several meaningful gaps in what the checks establish. I did **not** find a demonstrated shared-spec/RTL error in the sequencer fetch, FIFO full/pop, port-hold, or matcher logic from this read-only review; agreement between the two models alone does not settle those contracts.

## Findings

1. **High — SRAM macro behaviour is asserted, not checked against the behavioural RAM.**  
   [tt_top.ml:11-14](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/src/tt_top.ml:11) says the macros are equivalent to `Chip_rtl.behavioural_mem`, including write-through behaviour. The wrapper asserts both `A_REN` and `A_WEN` during writes ([tt_top.ml:20-33](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/src/tt_top.ml:20)), while the lockstep uses only behavioural memory. The macro probe results I found exercise a later read, not same-address read/write output semantics.  
   **Demonstrate:** simulate the IHP FUNCTIONAL macro and `behavioural_mem` side by side; write and read the same address on one clock, then compare outputs on that and the next clock. Also exercise the last address and reset/run transitions.

2. **High — Demo RTL is not independently connected to its own board and host.**  
   `run_board` compares pad nibbles and OE ([demos.ml:23-39](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/demos.ml:23)), but `Board.samples` advances one `Host.t` and resolves host data against `b.out`; `Board.advance` sets `b.out` from the specification core ([board.ml:38-64](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/src/board.ml:38)). Thus the RTL sees a board driven by specification outputs, not an independently resolved RTL board. This is useful output lockstep, but weaker than two end-to-end runs of the demo.  
   **Demonstrate:** run separate boards/hosts for spec and RTL, each resolving its own D/OE and recording host read bytes; compare those traces and verdicts.

3. **High — “unchanged demos” describes reused components, not unchanged end-to-end demos.**  
   The DS demo rebuilds and relocates firmware through `Compat` ([demos.ml:41-51](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/demos.ml:41)), then uses a new host and timing harness ([demos.ml:55-99](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/demos.ml:55)). Its sigrok trace is written only when a directory is supplied ([demos.ml:125-137](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/demos.ml:125)); sigrok is a separate invocation, though its recorded run passed and caught its planted faults ([results/sigrok-judge.txt:1-24](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/results/sigrok-judge.txt:1)). The DAC judge also fits a lead-in from 0 to 2000 steps and reports the best Hamming error ([demos.ml:231-255](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/demos.ml:231)).  
   **Demonstrate:** run `demos.exe ds` without a trace directory and observe that it does not invoke sigrok. For the DAC, report the full error-versus-lead curve and whether the zero-error lead is unique; inject a startup/frame displacement and check whether the fit absorbs it.

4. **Medium — Lockstep compares selected state, not all state or host read data as an independent stream.**  
   The comparator checks architectural sequencer/PE state and several assist outputs, but only FIFO **counts** for host FIFOs ([lockstep.ml:87-138](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/lockstep.ml:87)). It does not compare FIFO contents/order, streamer state, sampler FIFO contents, register-file values, fetch state, memory contents, matcher configuration, or NCO accumulator. Output comparison is pads plus `uio_oe` ([lockstep.ml:140-144](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/lockstep.ml:140)). A data corruption can therefore remain undetected until it changes a visible output.  
   **Demonstrate:** add a mutation that swaps two host-FIFO entries while preserving count, then observe that the state comparator reports no difference until a later consumer exposes it. The README’s “host link’s read data” claim ([README.md:160-163](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/README.md:160)) should be read as eventual pad-level comparison, not a separate per-clock read-data oracle.

5. **Medium — Random pin inputs never exercise pads 8–13 as external inputs.**  
   `ext_world` toggles only pads 0–7, 14, and 15 ([lockstep.ml:241-257](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/bin/lockstep.ml:241)). Selectors may map logic onto other pads, but those pads receive no external transitions from this generator. The reported 0 mismatches and planted-bug catches therefore do not establish those input paths.  
   **Demonstrate:** configure sequencer, sampler, and edge-sampler inputs to use each of pads 8–13, drive transitions there, and assert the intended sampled quarter values.

6. **Medium — Reset synchroniser behaviour at startup and with a stopped clock is untested.**  
   The two reset flops have no reset of their own ([tt_top.ml:43-46](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/src/tt_top.ml:43)). Reset is therefore not guaranteed to be asserted until two rising edges sample `rst_n` low; a pulse while the clock is stopped is missed. The README explains the both-edge approximation and its quarter-late output, but this hardened wrapper is not covered by the core lockstep.  
   **Demonstrate:** in a top-level simulation, pulse `rst_n` low and high while `clk` is stopped, then start the clock and inspect reset-dependent state. Also check startup before two rising edges after reset assertion.

## Claims to narrow

The root README says every output is compared and protocol traffic is judged independently ([README.md:112-126](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/README.md:112)); for this prototype, lockstep output means pad nibbles and OE, while state comparison is selective. The chip-top README’s “unchanged” demo wording ([README.md:167-170](/home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-top/prototypes/chip-top/README.md:167)) should distinguish reused firmware/decoders from the rewritten board harness, and say that sigrok is a separate recorded check. The DAC result is a zero-error best fit over a searched lead-in, rather than a fixed-alignment comparison.
